Surah: 93. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s093/surah.r2.hftbundle/text.md =====
# Surah 93

- 93:1 وَٱلضُّحَىٰ
- 93:2 وَٱلَّيْلِ إِذَا سَجَىٰ
- 93:3 مَا وَدَّعَكَ رَبُّكَ وَمَا قَلَىٰ
- 93:4 وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكَ مِنَ ٱلْأُولَىٰ
- 93:5 وَلَسَوْفَ يُعْطِيكَ رَبُّكَ فَتَرْضَىٰٓ
- 93:6 أَلَمْ يَجِدْكَ يَتِيمًۭا فَـَٔاوَىٰ
- 93:7 وَوَجَدَكَ ضَآلًّۭا فَهَدَىٰ
- 93:8 وَوَجَدَكَ عَآئِلًۭا فَأَغْنَىٰ
- 93:9 فَأَمَّا ٱلْيَتِيمَ فَلَا تَقْهَرْ
- 93:10 وَأَمَّا ٱلسَّآئِلَ فَلَا تَنْهَرْ
- 93:11 وَأَمَّا بِنِعْمَةِ رَبِّكَ فَحَدِّثْ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s093/surah.r2.hftbundle/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

# s093 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. Temporal Passage and Emergence
- Semantic invariant: Things occupy phases, move through ordered succession, and become manifest after absence.
- Surface relation: direct; 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`, 93:2 `ٱلَّيْلِ` and `سَجَىٰ`, 93:4 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ` and `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`, and 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`.
- Surprising reach: The day-night sequence extends into reversal, ontological emergence, fresh life, and sudden incident.

#### Subchannel A. Daylight, Night, and Stilling
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Bright daytime opens and extends, then night covers the scene and settles into stillness.
- Active motifs: extended forenoon `ض ح و:B001/m01`; clear forenoon brightness `ض ح و:B005/m01`; night and darkness `ل ي ل:B001/m01`; night settling into stillness `س ج و:B001/m01`; daylight opening `ن ه ر:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`; 93:2 `ٱلَّيْلِ`, `سَجَىٰ`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Forenoon supplies both duration and radiance, while night supplies darkness and `سَجَىٰ` supplies the act of becoming still. The lexical opening of daylight completes a cycle in which illumination expands, darkness arrives, and motion subsides.

#### Subchannel B. Firstness, Laterness, and Return
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A sequence begins, reaches a later phase, may be deferred, and turns back toward its outcome.
- Active motifs: first position or onset `ء و ل:B001/m01`; later or subsequent state `ء خ ر:B001/m01`; deferment `ء خ ر:B002/m01`; return to outcome `ء و ل:B002/m01`; backward movement `ق ه ر:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ`, `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Firstness and laterness establish ordered phases. Deferment lengthens the interval, while return to an eventual end and backward motion give that order a reversible shape rather than a static before-after contrast.

#### Subchannel C. Coming into Being and Disclosure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Something absent enters existence, is encountered as real, and is brought into view or speech.
- Active motifs: origination after nonbeing `ح د ث:B001/m01`; existent entity `و ج د:B002/m01`; manifestation and showing `ح د ث:B006/m01`; sudden incident `ح د ث:B005/m01`; initial emergence `ء و ل:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Beginning marks entry into a phase, occurrence marks entry into reality, existence makes the new thing available to encounter, and disclosure makes it publicly perceptible. A sudden incident is the disruptive form of the same emergence process.

#### Subchannel D. Freshness and Early Life
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A life or phase remains close to its beginning and carries the freshness of recent arrival.
- Active motifs: youth and recentness `ح د ث:B002/m01`; newly delivered ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`; fresh beginning or early youth `ر ب ب:B009/m02`; first phase `ء و ل:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Recent birth, youth, and firstness converge on a scene whose defining state is proximity to origin. Freshness is therefore temporal as well as bodily.

### 2. Orientation, Search, and Passage
- Semantic invariant: A participant moves relative to a course, loses or recovers orientation, and reaches either a destination or retreat.
- Surface relation: direct; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`, and 93:7 `ضَآلًّا` and `فَهَدَىٰ`.
- Surprising reach: Moral guidance opens into physical finding, gait, front-rear orientation, farewell, and refuge.

#### Subchannel A. Straying and Gentle Guidance
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A traveler departs from the intended course and is gently directed back toward the road, purpose, or truth.
- Active motifs: deviation from the way `ض ل ل:B001/m01`; gentle direction to road or truth `ه د ي:B001/m01`; course, manner, and intended direction `ه د ي:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:7 `ضَآلًّا`, `فَهَدَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Deviation supplies the displaced participant, guidance supplies the directing action, and the course or manner supplies the route that makes correction intelligible. The scene works equally as road-finding and restoration of purpose.

#### Subchannel B. Hidden, Lost, and Found
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An object disappears from view or memory, becomes hard to locate, and is recovered through finding.
- Active motifs: concealment or disappearance `ض ل ل:B002/m01`; lost object `ض ل ل:B003/m01`; forgotten object `ض ل ل:B004/m01`; finding and locating `و ج د:B001/m01`; directionless lost creature `ع ي ل:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:7 `ضَآلًّا`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`
- Synthesis: Disappearance, loss of location, and loss from memory are distinct ways an object becomes unavailable. Finding reverses each condition, while the directionless creature gives the search a mobile participant.

#### Subchannel C. Forward Motion and Agility
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A moving body rises lightly, advances from the front, and proceeds on foot.
- Active motifs: light rising or swift advance `ق ل ي:B001/m01`; pedestrian movement `ن ع م:B012/m01`; leading front `ه د ي:B003/m01`; rear or hindpart `ء خ ر:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3 `قَلَىٰ`; 93:4 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Lightness initiates advance, the leading front gives it orientation, walking realizes the passage, and the hindpart establishes the axis along which forward movement can be recognized.

#### Subchannel D. Swaying, Support, and Delayed Pace
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A weak or burdened walker advances slowly, sways, and may depend on companions for support.
- Active motifs: swaying gait `ع ي ل:B003/m01`; supported swaying walk `ه د ي:B008/m01`; delayed progress `ي ت م:B004/m01`; composed unhurried bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; overpowering impediment `ع ي ل:B009/m01`; weak or heavy participant `ه د ي:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`
- Synthesis: Weakness and impediment slow the traveler; bodily swaying shows the unstable gait; reliance on others supplies a walking mechanism; and composure converts slowness from mere incapacity into measured bearing.

#### Subchannel E. Farewell, Retreat, and Refuge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A traveler departs with farewell, may reverse course, and finally joins a sheltering destination.
- Active motifs: farewell at departure `و د ع:B003/m01`; night travel `ل ي ل:B002/m01`; backward retreat `ق ه ر:B005/m01`; return toward an end `ء و ل:B002/m01`; joining a refuge `ء و ي:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `ٱلَّيْلِ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Farewell begins the journey, night supplies its setting, retreat reverses its direction, and return gives the movement an endpoint. Refuge closes the passage by gathering the traveler into a protected place.

### 3. Care, Need, and Provision
- Semantic invariant: Vulnerability is encountered and answered through shelter, nurture, provision, and responsive giving rather than neglect or rebuke.
- Surface relation: direct; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`, 93:6 `يَتِيمًا` and `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`, 93:8 `عَآئِلًا` and `فَأَغْنَىٰ`, 93:9 `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`, 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ` and `تَنْهَرْ`, and 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`.
- Surprising reach: The surface sequence of rescue and command expands into a full economy of guardianship, sufficiency, fulfilled requests, and anti-neglect.

#### Subchannel A. The Orphan Received into Care
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A child lacking a protector is found, sheltered, and raised under continuing care.
- Active motifs: orphaned child `ي ت م:B001/m01`; finding the vulnerable person `و ج د:B001/m01`; sheltering another `ء و ي:B001/m02`; gradual nurture `ر ب ب:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`
- Synthesis: Orphanhood names the broken protective relation, finding brings the child into view, sheltering restores place and belonging, and gradual nurture turns a moment of rescue into sustained care.

#### Subchannel B. Poverty Changed into Sufficiency
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A person in need receives enough resources and capacity to leave dependence behind.
- Active motifs: poverty and need `ع ي ل:B001/m01`; poor nourishment `ع ي ل:B004/m01`; wealth or independence `غ ن ي:B001/m01`; sufficiency and useful adequacy `غ ن ي:B002/m01`; material capacity `و ج د:B003/m01`; good condition `ن ع م:B001/m01`; state or circumstance `ء و ل:B007/m01`; need `ر ب ب:B016/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`, `فَأَغْنَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Poverty and inadequate nourishment define the initial circumstance. Wealth, capacity, and practical sufficiency are distinct outcomes: possession increases, need falls, and the person becomes able to stand without dependence.

#### Subchannel C. Request, Grant, and Gentle Reception
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A requester names a need, seeks a gift, receives a grant, and is not driven away by harsh speech.
- Active motifs: asking and seeking `س ء ل:B001/m01`; desired object `س ء ل:B002/m01`; fulfillment of a request `س ء ل:B003/m01`; giving `ع ط و:B002/m01`; solicitation for a gift `ع ط و:B005/m01`; generous gift `خ ي ر:B005/m01`; affectionate present `ه د ي:B004/m01`; harsh rebuke `ن ه ر:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ`, `تَنْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: The requested object gives asking a goal, solicitation initiates transfer, giving and the affectionate present answer it, and fulfillment completes it. Harsh rebuke is the rejected counter-action that would break this exchange at its opening.

#### Subchannel D. Stewardship against Coercion and Neglect
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A responsible holder governs and repairs what is under care instead of abandoning, ignoring, or overpowering it.
- Active motifs: lordship and responsible possession `ر ب ب:B001/m01`; governance and reform `ء و ل:B004/m01`; abandonment `و د ع:B001/m01`; neglect or omission `ي ت م:B003/m01`; refusal to touch or attend `س ج و:B004/m01`; coercive domination `ق ه ر:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `رَبُّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:5 and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ`, `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Lordship and governance establish responsibility for another's condition. Abandonment, non-attendance, and neglect withdraw that responsibility, while coercion converts it into harmful power. Repair is the positive operation that opposes both failures.

### 4. Household, Estate, and Custody
- Semantic invariant: People, property, and dependents are gathered into durable relations of maintenance, service, guardianship, and entrusted keeping.
- Surface relation: indirect; 93:3, 93:5, and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`, 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا` and `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`, and 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`.
- Surprising reach: Care expands from the orphan into family organization, foster relations, marriage, estate labor, and deposit custody.

#### Subchannel A. Household, Dependents, and Service
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A household head gathers family and followers, maintains dependents, and assigns service for their needs.
- Active motifs: household and retinue `ء و ل:B003/m01`; dependents `ع ي ل:B002/m01`; service and handing what is needed `ع ط و:B003/m01`; labor placed at another's disposal `و د ع:B009/m01`; gathered multitude `ر ب ب:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`
- Synthesis: The household supplies the social container, dependents supply those maintained within it, and service supplies the repeated transfer of labor and necessities. The multitude motif scales the same relation from one family to a gathered group.

#### Subchannel B. Foster Parent and Ward
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A young dependent from an earlier household is received by a foster parent or nurse and raised as a ward.
- Active motifs: stepchild or ward `ر ب ب:B005/m01`; foster parent, nurse, or caregiver `ر ب ب:B005/m02`; orphaned child `ي ت م:B001/m01`; youthful recent age `ح د ث:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Youth and orphanhood describe the ward's vulnerability, while the paired ward-caregiver senses supply a replacement relation of nurture. The scene centers on social incorporation rather than biological descent.

#### Subchannel C. Bride, Spouse, and Marital Independence
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A woman moves from singleness into marriage, is conducted to a spouse, and gains a recognized household position.
- Active motifs: bride conducted to her spouse `ه د ي:B006/m01`; married or self-sufficient woman `غ ن ي:B005/m01`; marriage as protection from singleness `غ ن ي:B006/m01`; woman apart from a husband `ي ت م:B005/m01`; personal beauty or excellence `خ ي ر:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:8 `فَأَغْنَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Singleness supplies the initial social state, bridal conduct supplies the transition, marriage supplies the new bond, and marital sufficiency names its protective or independent outcome. Beauty enters as a personal quality within that marital scene.

#### Subchannel D. Estate Repair and Directed Labor
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A holder directly manages an estate, repairs its condition, and deploys labor to keep it productive.
- Active motifs: direct management of an estate `س ج و:B005/m01`; estate ordering and repair `ء و ل:B004/m02`; repair, completion, and oversight `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; labor made available for a task `و د ع:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Direct management identifies the setting, ordering supplies administrative control, repair restores the estate's condition, and deployed labor carries the work into practice.

#### Subchannel E. Deposit and Safekeeping
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Property is handed over, held under another's care, maintained, and returned at a later time.
- Active motifs: entrusted deposit `و د ع:B005/m01`; custodial oversight and repair `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; ordered estate keeping `ء و ل:B004/m02`; deferred transfer `ء خ ر:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `رَبُّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ`, `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:5 and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ`, `رَبِّكَ`
- Synthesis: Deposit creates the custodial relation, oversight preserves the entrusted object, estate ordering keeps it within an accountable arrangement, and deferment explains why possession and final transfer occur at different times.

### 5. Covenant, Assent, and Power
- Semantic invariant: Social relations are stabilized through pact, consent, protection, and surety, or ruptured through coercion and hostile dominance.
- Surface relation: direct; 93:3 `قَلَىٰ`, 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`, and 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`.
- Surprising reach: Satisfaction unfolds into bilateral accord, legal guarantee, protected status, truce, and contested victory.

#### Subchannel A. Pact, Truce, and Protected Entrant
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Parties suspend conflict through a pact and extend protected standing to a person entering their domain.
- Active motifs: covenant or pact `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; truce and mutual cessation `و د ع:B004/m01`; protected entrant or asylum seeker `ه د ي:B007/m01`; captive under another's control `ه د ي:B007/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `رَبُّكَ`; 93:5 and 93:11 `رَبُّكَ`, `رَبِّكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`
- Synthesis: The pact binds the parties, truce names the cessation it produces, and protected status applies that bond to a vulnerable entrant. The captive sense marks the neighboring condition in which protection and control must be distinguished.

#### Subchannel B. Acceptance and Mutual Pleasing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Aversion is removed, acceptance is established, and two parties make their approval reciprocal.
- Active motifs: acceptance opposed to displeasure `ر ض و:B001/m01`; abundant or sought satisfaction `ر ض و:B002/m01`; mutual accord `ر ض و:B003/m01`; active pleasing of another `ر ض و:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Acceptance supplies an inward state, sought satisfaction makes it a goal, pleasing makes one party act toward the other, and mutual accord closes the relation bilaterally.

#### Subchannel C. Obedience, Attachment, and Surety
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A trusted subordinate is attached to a principal through willing obedience, affection, or guarantee.
- Active motifs: obedient trusted person `ر ض و:B006/m01`; loving attached person `ر ض و:B006/m02`; guarantor or surety `ر ض و:B006/m03`; lord or principal `ر ب ب:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`
- Synthesis: The principal supplies the relation's authoritative pole. Obedience realizes it behaviorally, attachment realizes it affectively, and surety realizes it as an obligation undertaken for another.

#### Subchannel D. Coercion, Contest, and Cessation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Opponents drive, seize, or overpower one another until conflict is restrained by cessation.
- Active motifs: coercive domination `ق ه ر:B001/m01`; victory in contest `ع ط و:B007/m01`; overcoming a rival `ر ض و:B005/m01`; expulsion or driving out `ق ل ي:B002/m01`; stealthy snatching `ن ه ر:B006/m01`; armistice `و د ع:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, `قَلَىٰ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`, `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Driving and snatching initiate dispossession, contest turns it into reciprocal struggle, and coercion fixes a victor over a subdued party. Armistice is the social mechanism that interrupts this chain without requiring either side's annihilation.

### 6. Voice, Dialogue, and Public Memory
- Semantic invariant: Speech moves between participants, discloses what matters, and can persist as interpretation, reputation, song, or remembered account.
- Surface relation: direct; 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ` and 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`.
- Surprising reach: The command to recount expands into dialogue, public reputation, learned interpretation, and performed verse.

#### Subchannel A. Question, Answer, and Mutual Consultation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Participants ask one another, answer affirmatively, exchange news, and reach an agreed response.
- Active motifs: reciprocal questioning `س ء ل:B004/m01`; affirmative answer `ن ع م:B004/m01`; renewed speech or news `ح د ث:B003/m01`; mutual accord `ر ض و:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`, `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Reciprocal questioning opens the exchange, renewed speech supplies its content, affirmation answers it, and mutual accord gives the conversation a shared outcome.

#### Subchannel B. Telling the Benefaction
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A received good is made visible by being recounted as news.
- Active motifs: narrated speech `ح د ث:B003/m01`; manifestation and showing `ح د ث:B006/m01`; bestowed benefaction `ن ع م:B001/m02`; generous gift `خ ي ر:B005/m01`; lordly giver `ر ب ب:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`, `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Benefaction supplies the content, the giver supplies its relational source, narration turns it into speech, and manifestation lets the spoken good become publicly perceptible.

#### Subchannel C. Conduct Becoming Public Memory
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person's course of action is repeatedly discussed until it becomes a remembered story or reputation.
- Active motifs: becoming an anecdote or public account `ح د ث:B004/m01`; course and manner `ه د ي:B002/m01`; lapse or omission `ي ت م:B003/m01`; gathered public `ر ب ب:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Conduct supplies the subject of talk, a lapse supplies a memorable deviation, the gathered public repeats it, and repeated speech converts the person or event into an enduring anecdote.

#### Subchannel D. Interpretation as Return to Meaning
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Speech is carried back to its intended sense under learned direction.
- Active motifs: interpretation by return to meaning `ء و ل:B002/m02`; speech or report `ح د ث:B003/m01`; directed course of discourse `ه د ي:B002/m01`; learned teacher or sage `ر ب ب:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: A report presents language that can travel away from its intended point. Interpretation returns it to that point, while the learned guide supplies the disciplined course by which the return is made.

#### Subchannel E. Song and Exchanged Verse
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A voice performs melody or verse and directs the composition toward another person, who may answer in kind.
- Active motifs: singing and melody `غ ن ي:B003/m01`; directed poem or poetic exchange `ه د ي:B011/m01`; spoken composition `ح د ث:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:8 `فَأَغْنَىٰ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: Speech provides verbal content, singing gives it performed sound, and poetic direction gives it an addressee. Exchange turns the performance into a reciprocal verbal encounter.

### 7. Residence, Ease, and Composure
- Semantic invariant: A participant settles into a fitting place or bodily state and gains stability, comfort, and quiet bearing.
- Surface relation: indirect; 93:2 `سَجَىٰ`, 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`, 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`, 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`, and 93:8 `فَأَغْنَىٰ`.
- Surprising reach: Shelter becomes both a physical abode and a manner of settled softness, flexibility, and dignity.

#### Subchannel A. Shelter and Agreeable Abode
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person joins a shelter, remains there, and finds the place sufficient and agreeable for habitation.
- Active motifs: joining a refuge `ء و ي:B001/m01`; lasting residence `ر ب ب:B007/m01`; inhabited abode `غ ن ي:B004/m01`; agreeable place and pleasant stay `ن ع م:B011/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`; 93:8 `فَأَغْنَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Refuge supplies entry, residence supplies duration, the abode supplies location, and agreeableness turns mere occupancy into a fitting home.

#### Subchannel B. Rest, Softness, and Quiet Bearing
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Effort subsides, the body or material yields without resistance, and the participant holds a calm, dignified posture.
- Active motifs: repose and ease `و د ع:B002/m01`; softness and refined comfort `ن ع م:B002/m01`; calm bearing `ه د ي:B010/m01`; eyelid stillness `س ج و:B001/m04`; flexibility and yielding `ع ط و:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Repose removes exertion, softness and flexibility remove resistance, eyelid stillness gives the calm a bodily sign, and composed bearing makes comfort socially visible as dignity.

### 8. Weather, Water, and Navigation
- Semantic invariant: Air, cloud, light, and flowing water form an environment through which land, vegetation, and travelers are sustained or directed.
- Surface relation: indirect; 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`, 93:2 `ٱلَّيْلِ` and `سَجَىٰ`, and 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`.
- Surprising reach: Night stillness and daylight open into cloud layers, south wind, river cutting, persistent greenery, calm sea, and maritime command.

#### Subchannel A. Layered Cloud, Soft Wind, and Green Persistence
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Clouds gather and linger, a soft moist wind moves beneath them, and vegetation remains green.
- Active motifs: layered raincloud `ر ب ب:B008/m01`; cloud mass `ن ه ر:B008/m01`; lingering weather `ر ب ب:B007/m02`; soft south wind `ن ع م:B009/m01`; persistent green plant `ر ب ب:B012/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Layering gives the cloud mass, lingering gives it duration, and the soft wind gives the weather motion without violence. The green plant is the durable terrestrial outcome of that sustained atmosphere.

#### Subchannel B. River, Current, and Opened Ground
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Abundant water cuts through land, opens a channel, and continues as a broad current.
- Active motifs: flowing river `ن ه ر:B001/m01`; opening and widening a channel `ن ه ر:B003/m01`; abundant gathered water `ر ب ب:B013/m01`; exposed daylight `ض ح و:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`; 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Abundant water supplies the material, opening and widening supply the geomorphic action, and the river is the stable path produced by that action. Daylight exposes the channel as an open feature of the land.

#### Subchannel C. Calm Sea and Maritime Command
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A captain directs travel across a calm sea beneath night and cloud.
- Active motifs: still sea `س ج و:B001/m02`; captain of sailors `ر ب ب:B017/m01`; cloud overhead `ن ه ر:B008/m01`; travel by night `ل ي ل:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `ٱلَّيْلِ`, `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Sea stillness supplies navigable conditions, night supplies the travel setting, cloud supplies the changing sky, and the captain supplies organized direction across the water.

### 9. Food and Material Transformation
- Semantic invariant: Materials change through thickening, heat, abrasion, washing, and polishing.
- Surface relation: none.
- Surprising reach: Roots heard in sequence, lordship, hatred, power, and narration also describe a practical workshop of pastes, cooked food, grinding, cleanser, and polish.

#### Subchannel A. Thickening and Coagulation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A liquid or paste loses fluidity, gathers into a denser mass, and can be used as a prepared substance.
- Active motifs: liquid curdling or congealing `ء و ل:B005/m01`; thick syrup or paste `ر ب ب:B006/m01`; tightly bound knot or mass `ر ب ب:B016/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Curdling supplies the transition, syrup or paste supplies the dense material state, and the tight knot supplies the endpoint of cohesion. Together they describe consolidation from fluidity to a bound mass.

#### Subchannel B. Frying, Boiling, and the Prepared Meal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Food is exposed to heat, releases or absorbs liquid, thickens into a dish, and is served as a meal.
- Active motifs: frying on a pan `ق ل ي:B004/m01`; meat altered by heat `ق ه ر:B002/m01`; hot-stone dairy and flour dish `ق ه ر:B003/m01`; morning meal `ض ح و:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`; 93:3 `قَلَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: Frying provides direct pan heat, heated meat shows moisture leaving under fire, and the hot-stone mixture shows boiling and stirring into a finished preparation. The morning meal gives the transformed food its serving context.

#### Subchannel C. Grinding, Washing, and Polishing
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hard or alkaline implement abrades residue from a surface until the object becomes clean or bright.
- Active motifs: hard grinding stone `ق ه ر:B004/m01`; cleansing alkali or ash `ق ل ي:B006/m01`; polishing a blade `ح د ث:B007/m01`; polishing a heart through admonition `ح د ث:B007/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3 `قَلَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`; 93:11 `فَحَدِّثْ`
- Synthesis: The stone supplies mechanical abrasion, alkali supplies chemical cleansing, and polishing supplies the brightened outcome. The heart sense preserves the same removal-of-rust mechanism while transferring it from metal to moral interiority.

### 10. Holding, Covering, and Carrying
- Semantic invariant: Objects are kept together, protected from exposure, or borne by a supporting frame.
- Surface relation: none.
- Surprising reach: Lexical senses form a material system of storage vessels, clustered contents, shrouds, protective cloth, carriers, and projecting supports.

#### Subchannel A. Vessels and Clustered Contents
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A container gathers arrows, lots, drink, or stacked provisions and preserves their ordered mass.
- Active motifs: leather container or quiver `ر ب ب:B010/m01`; gathered lots or arrows `ر ب ب:B010/m02`; drink-maturing vessel `ء و ل:B010/m01`; stacked provisions in a container `ق ه ر:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: The leather case gathers discrete shafts or lots, the drink vessel holds liquid through time, and the provision container fixes food in stacked order. Their common operation is bounded keeping.

#### Subchannel B. Wrapping, Shrouding, and Concealment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Cloth is extended over an object or body, protects what lies beneath it, and removes it from view.
- Active motifs: covering with a cloth `س ج و:B002/m01`; protective outer garment `و د ع:B006/m01`; burial beneath view `ض ل ل:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:7 `ضَآلًّا`
- Synthesis: Covering supplies the immediate action, the outer garment supplies a protective layer, and burial completes concealment by placing the covered body beyond ordinary sight.

#### Subchannel C. Carrier, Bier, and Supporting Frame
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A rigid frame bears a singular load and presents a leading or projecting part for movement.
- Active motifs: carrier or bier `ء و ل:B008/m01`; structural timbers or carrying tool `ء و ل:B008/m02`; singular unmatched object `ي ت م:B002/m01`; leading projection or arrowhead `ه د ي:B003/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Timbers form the supporting frame, the bier gives that frame a carrying function, the singular object supplies its load, and the leading projection gives the assembly a directed front.

### 11. Herds, Wildlife, and Ritual Gifts
- Semantic invariant: Animals are gathered, grazed, lost, sheltered, bred, hunted, or transferred as offerings.
- Surface relation: indirect; 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`, 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`, 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا` and `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`, and 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`.
- Surprising reach: Human vulnerability and guidance extend into pastoral ownership, wildlife refuge, sacrifice, bird form, and herd dispersal.

#### Subchannel A. Herd, Grazing, and the Stray
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A herd grazes early, depends on an owner, produces breeding stock, and risks losing animals to the open waste.
- Active motifs: gathered herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; livestock `ن ع م:B005/m01`; early grazing `ض ح و:B003/m02`; stray animal `ض ل ل:B005/m01`; directionless stray `ع ي ل:B008/m01`; breeding male retained for the herd `و د ع:B008/m01`; motherless animal `ي ت م:B001/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`; 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`; 93:7 `ضَآلًّا`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Herd and livestock establish collective ownership, early grazing establishes routine, and the retained male sustains reproduction. Straying breaks the owner-animal relation, while motherlessness creates the animal form of lost care.

#### Subchannel B. Offering and Sacrifice
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Livestock is selected, transferred toward a sacred destination, and slaughtered as an offering.
- Active motifs: ritual offering sent to the sanctuary `ه د ي:B005/m01`; sacrificial animal `ض ح و:B004/m01`; livestock fit for offering `ن ع م:B005/m01`; transfer as gift `ع ط و:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:1 `ٱلضُّحَىٰ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Livestock supplies the object, selection as a sacrificial animal gives it ritual status, transfer directs it to the sanctuary, and giving explains the offering relation.

#### Subchannel C. Wildlife, Lure, and Refuge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Wild animals move between exposed ground, concealed dens, and mountain refuge while a hunter attempts to draw them out.
- Active motifs: ibex or deer seeking mountain refuge `ء و ل:B009/m01`; male hyena `ع ي ل:B007/m01`; lure placed at a burrow `خ ي ر:B006/m01`; refuge and defensive gathering `ء و ي:B001/m01`; small wild creature `ق ه ر:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`, `خَيْرٌ`; 93:6 `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`; 93:9 `تَقْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: The den and mountain provide two forms of refuge, the wild animals supply the mobile participants, and the lure attempts to reverse concealment by forcing emergence into exposed ground.

#### Subchannel D. Ostrich Form and Dispersal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The ostrich supplies a visible body pattern that is transferred to tools and landmarks, then to the image of a group scattering in flight.
- Active motifs: ostrich `ن ع م:B006/m01`; ostrich-shaped structures and markers `ن ع م:B007/m01`; ostrich flight as group dispersal `ن ع م:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: The bird is the concrete model, shape analogy transfers its body to built or natural forms, and the flight expression transfers its motion to a defeated or dispersing group.

### 12. Goodness, Choice, and Flourishing
- Semantic invariant: A beneficial state is recognized, chosen, cultivated, increased, and received with satisfaction.
- Surface relation: direct; 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`, 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`, and 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`.
- Surprising reach: Evaluative goodness becomes a process involving selection, temperament, nurture, increase, and delight.

#### Subchannel A. Benefit and Excellence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person or thing is repaired toward a useful condition and recognized for goodness, merit, or beauty.
- Active motifs: beneficial good `خ ي ر:B001/m01`; excellence and merit `خ ي ر:B002/m01`; good condition `ن ع م:B001/m01`; repair and completion `ر ب ب:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Repair produces a sound condition, benefit describes its practical value, excellence describes its comparative merit, and good condition names the flourishing result.

#### Subchannel B. Selection and the Desired End
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A chooser identifies a desired outcome, compares alternatives, and settles on the option that brings satisfaction.
- Active motifs: choosing or seeking the better option `خ ي ر:B003/m01`; desired object `س ء ل:B002/m01`; outcome toward which a matter returns `ء و ل:B002/m01`; acceptance of the result `ر ض و:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`, `خَيْرٌ`; 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:10 `ٱلسَّآئِلَ`
- Synthesis: Desire supplies a goal, comparison supplies alternatives, choice directs action toward one of them, outcome reveals what the choice becomes, and acceptance closes the process.

#### Subchannel C. Disposition Cultivated into Virtue
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An innate disposition is gradually trained and softened until it displays refined excellence.
- Active motifs: natural temperament `س ج و:B003/m01`; gradual nurture `ر ب ب:B002/m02`; cultivated excellence `خ ي ر:B002/m01`; softness and refinement `ن ع م:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `سَجَىٰ`; 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `خَيْرٌ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Temperament supplies the starting nature, nurture supplies repeated formation, softness marks responsiveness to training, and excellence is the stable character produced.

#### Subchannel D. Blessing, Increase, and Delight
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A benefaction is enlarged until it produces abundant satisfaction and delight.
- Active motifs: bestowed blessing `ن ع م:B001/m02`; increase or intensification `ن ع م:B010/m01`; delight and solace `ن ع م:B013/m01`; blessing or favor `ر ب ب:B016/m03`; abundant satisfaction `ر ض و:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: Favor initiates the good, increase enlarges it, delight is its experienced effect, and abundant satisfaction is the settled outcome.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. Quantity, Praise, and Assent
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small grammatical forms regulate quantity, evaluation, and affirmation within discourse.
- Active motifs: approximating or quantifying particle `ر ب ب:B015/m01`; praise construction `ن ع م:B003/m01`; affirmative answer `ن ع م:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`
- Synthesis: The particle frames an amount or frequency, the praise form assigns positive value, and the affirmative form commits the speaker to a proposition. Their shared scene is discourse-level stance rather than concrete reference.

### S2. Names and Appellations
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Lexical material is detached from ordinary description and assigned as a personal, geographic, poetic, or conventional name.
- Active motifs: Mount Radwa `ر ض و:B007/m01`; feminine personal names `ر ض و:B007/m02`; Layla and a wine appellation `ل ي ل:B004/m01`; Aylan as name or title `ع ي ل:B006/m01`; named persons, places, or stars `ن ه ر:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:2 `ٱلَّيْلِ`; 93:5 `فَتَرْضَىٰ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`; 93:10 `تَنْهَرْ`
- Synthesis: The motifs share the act of designation: a root-derived form becomes a stable identifier for a person, place, object, or celestial marker rather than a transient description.

### S3. Cowrie Shell Ornament
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small white shells are taken from the sea and hung on an object as decoration.
- Active motifs: marine cowrie shell `و د ع:B007/m01`; suspended shell ornament `و د ع:B007/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3 `وَدَّعَكَ`
- Synthesis: The sea supplies the material object, its small white form supplies visual appeal, and suspension converts the shell into an ornament.

### S4. Lots, Projectiles, and the Stick Game
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Handled shafts are gathered in a case, selected or projected, and echoed by a game in which one stick strikes another.
- Active motifs: clustered lots or arrows `ر ب ب:B010/m02`; quiver or lot container `ر ب ب:B010/m01`; stick-striking game `ق ل ي:B005/m01`; arrowhead or leading point `ه د ي:B003/m02`; grasping by hand `ع ط و:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:3 `قَلَىٰ`; 93:5 `يُعْطِيكَ`; 93:7 `فَهَدَىٰ`
- Synthesis: The container gathers the shafts, the hand selects and handles them, the point gives directional projection, and the stick game reproduces the same striking geometry in play.

### S5. Singularity and Otherness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An existent stands apart from peers because it lacks a match or is classified as other.
- Active motifs: singular unmatched entity `ي ت م:B002/m01`; other or non-first entity `ء خ ر:B001/m02`; existent thing `و ج د:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 93:4 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:6 and 93:9 `يَتِيمًا`, `ٱلْيَتِيمَ`
- Synthesis: Existence supplies the entity, otherness distinguishes it relationally, and singularity marks the stronger condition in which no peer or counterpart remains.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s093/surah.r2.hftbundle/hft.md =====
# HFT: earlier activation hypotheses, per focus ayah of surah 93

Note: HFT used an older root map; a trace step on a root the gateway now withholds is an echo, not identity.

# Focus 93:1

## duha extended ascent
- reading: An oath by daylight already risen and still unfolding, a span in which delayed things can mature.
- mechanism: The oath fixes attention on an interval that has risen and continues. Duration is part of the image, so the moment can carry maturation and the arrival of what was delayed.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B001: Risen forenoon daylight extending through the day supplies the model's sustained temporal span.

## duha public exposure
- reading: The forenoon is the condition in which things become exposed, prominent, and publicly legible.
- mechanism: Light is a field of manifestation: what had no visible standing comes out into an exposed, shared arena. The oath concerns appearance and publicity as much as luminosity.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Sun-exposure and visible prominence turn forenoon into an arena of disclosure and public standing.

## duha provisioning window
- reading: The named time is a daily provisioning window structured by hunger, movement, and nourishment.
- mechanism: The time is organized by embodied need: animals go out, food is sought, and a meal is taken. The oath can therefore evoke a recurring window in which life obtains provision.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B003: Forenoon eating and early grazing supply a material cycle of seeking and receiving nourishment.

## duha clear legibility
- reading: Duha is brightness as clarification: the world becomes distinguishable enough to read and navigate.
- mechanism: Brightness removes visual ambiguity and gives contours to what is present. The oath can foreground a condition of discernibility, not simply a quantity of light.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B005: Clear forenoon brightness supplies the mechanism by which obscured forms become distinct and readable.

## duha gentle delay
- reading: The oath can carry a patient tempo: what is delayed may be handled gently rather than abandoned.
- mechanism: The interval itself models patient handling. Delay need not be neglect; it can be the gentle pacing by which an outcome is allowed to arrive without force.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B006: Gentle slowing and respite make forenoon a temporal image of non-hasty care.

## cycle visibility and cover
- reading: Duha is the returning visible phase of a rhythm that also contains stillness and covering; its force does not require continuous exposure.
- mechanism: The oath-pair places exposed, extending forenoon beside night in its settling and covering phase. Visibility and concealment become alternating modes in one rhythm; the cessation of exposure is not the cessation of the cycle.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B001: Extended risen daylight supplies the visible phase that can recur after a settled interval.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Open prominence supplies the exposed pole whose temporary withdrawal needs interpretation.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night as the darkness contrary to day supplies the alternating, non-visible pole.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settled stillness under the enclosing night makes concealment a repose rather than a rupture.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth sharpens the image of light being veiled without being destroyed.

## pause not relational exit
- reading: The delayed or veiled light becomes a test case for pause without rejection: obscurity does not entail that care has exited.
- mechanism: The denial of leaving and aversion, framed by enduring nurture, converts the gentle-delay baseline into a relational diagnostic. A quiet interval can be repose, but it is not abandonment, expulsion, or the heart's withdrawal.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B006: Gentle delay supplies a positive account of temporal pause before later denials exclude harsher accounts.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and relinquishing supply the rupture that the verse explicitly negates.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B002: Ease and stillness preserve a non-hostile sense of pause once abandonment is denied.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repair, and completion make the continuing relation developmental rather than merely possessive.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Remaining, dwelling, and duration supply continuity beneath the apparent interval.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Aversion that distances the heart supplies the rejected affective explanation for obscurity.

## delayed brightness as better later
- reading: Duha's extension is directional: a first condition can pass continuously into a later condition that carries greater good.
- mechanism: Later, beneficial good, beginning, outcome, and unbroken succession turn extended duha into directed maturation. Its rise is not neutral duration but a trajectory in which an initial state gives way to a more beneficial later disclosure.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B001: Daylight rising and extending supplies a natural process with distinguishable earlier and later phases.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B006: Gentle postponement makes the superior later phase compatible with present waiting.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the temporal displacement beyond the present phase.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good assigns positive direction rather than mere lateness to the movement.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence establish the initial phase from which the comparison departs.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an outcome or consequence gives the temporal extension a telos.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant split image of one thing following another supplies continuity between phases without a vacant gap.

## forenoon as gift maturation
- reading: Duha becomes a promise-shaped provisioning interval in which what is given is nurtured until it becomes satisfying.
- mechanism: The meal-and-grazing branch joins hand-to-hand giving, nurturing growth, and eventual satisfaction. Future giving fits the extended interval: provision arrives, is taken in, and matures into enough rather than appearing as an inert object.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B003: Forenoon feeding and grazing supply the material scene in which a gift becomes sustaining provision.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B001: The extending daytime interval gives provision time to arrive and have an effect.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Passing something to another supplies the concrete transfer at the start of the provisioning chain.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing and bringing to completion turn transfer into sustained development.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant split image of feeding and growth intensifies the maturation mechanism.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B002: Abundant or sought satisfaction supplies the experienced completion toward which the gift develops.

## visibility finds and shelters
- reading: Daylight finds isolated vulnerability so that exposure can terminate in refuge and belonging.
- mechanism: Public visibility becomes ethically productive when finding an isolated, unguarded person leads to gathering shelter and compassion. Light is a search field whose proper endpoint is enclosure and belonging, not exposure for its own sake.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Open visibility supplies the condition in which an otherwise isolated person can be noticed.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Encountering and locating something turns visibility into an act of finding.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child cut off from a guardian supplies the vulnerable state that visibility discovers.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Singularity and separation widen orphanhood into a structural image of isolation.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering refuge supplies the spatial reversal from exposed isolation to shelter.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy supplies the affective quality that makes shelter more than containment.

## brightness as wayfinding
- reading: Duha is operative wayfinding: it brings the unlocated into view and gives that visibility a direction.
- mechanism: Clear prominence becomes navigational: a hidden or lost state is found and then gently directed. Daylight does not merely display objects; it yields a path, a leading edge, and an orientation.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B005: Clear brightness supplies enough distinction for orientation rather than mere visibility.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into visible prominence supplies the transition out of hiddenness.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding what was not securely located initiates the recovery mechanism.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness and disappearance supply an optical-spatial account of being unlocated.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B003: Loss supplies the condition that finding and direction reverse.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies non-coercive direction after discovery.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B003: A guide moving at the front gives the visible field a leading edge and destination.

## forenoon sufficiency ecology
- reading: The oath bears a recurring anti-scarcity pattern: embodied lack is met until there is enough to sustain life.
- mechanism: The forenoon meal and grazing image joins poverty and poor nourishment to capacity and sufficiency. Duha becomes a recurrent conversion of lack into enough, an ecology of support rather than a display of accumulated wealth.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B003: Early feeding and grazing supply the daily material operation through which lack can be answered.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B003: Capacity, means, and wealth supply the resource state discovered or produced in the reversal.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the deficit from which the provisioning sequence begins.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B004: Poor nourishment makes the deficit bodily and connects it directly to the focus's feeding branch.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency and adequacy define the endpoint as enoughness rather than excess.

## public light morally ambivalent
- reading: Prominence is morally ambivalent: full visibility can enable care or vertical crushing, so the daylight oath places publicity under non-domination.
- mechanism: Exposure is dangerous under hierarchy. An unguarded, isolated person under an overpowering force from above turns public visibility into an ethical test: prominence can notice and protect, or it can make vulnerability easier to crush.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Exposure and public prominence supply the morally ambivalent field in which power becomes visible and actionable.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Severance from a guardian supplies the asymmetrically exposed person lacking protective backing.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Dominance descending from above and humiliating its object supplies the abusive use of superior standing.

## daylight as open request channel
- reading: Duha's opening becomes interpersonal permeability: make room for requests, including quietly emerging ones, and do not dam them with harsh speech.
- mechanism: The نهر inventory creates an internal reversal among day opening, widening until flow, and harsh rebuke. Alongside overt request and the split image of gentle hidden extraction, duha becomes an open channel through which even indirect need can enter; scolding blocks that flow.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Open public appearance supplies the interpersonal space in which a need can become present to another.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking and requesting supply the explicit movement of need toward a listener.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The non-dominant split image of gentle, hidden drawing suggests that some requests emerge indirectly and must be noticed without force.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B002: Day opening in brightness directly links the response-space back to the focus's daylight.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until something flows supply the channel mechanics of social receptivity.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the closing force that the prohibition removes from the channel.

## brightness must become testimony
- reading: Duha is a disclosure cycle: care becomes visible, and visible benefit becomes renewed testimony rather than remaining mute.
- mechanism: Good condition and nurturing completion culminate in renewed speech and disclosure. Public daylight is not self-contained display: what care has brought into manifest form is to be manifested again as narration.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Visible prominence supplies the focus-side movement from hidden condition into public manifestation.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: A good, benefited condition supplies the content that has become manifest and can be recounted.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B003: Praising something with نعم gives narration an affirmative evaluative register.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing and completion identify care, rather than self-display, as the source of the visible good.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech continually renewed as news turns received benefit into an ongoing verbal event.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something out and making it appear mirrors the focus's visible disclosure in speech.

## sacrificial gift horizon
- reading: At the root-image margin, the marked time also opens an offering economy in which a gift is directed toward a sacred end.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B004: The Adha sacrifice supplies a root-internal horizon of something offered up at a marked time.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Concrete giving supplies the transfer that lets sacrifice be modeled as offering rather than loss alone.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B005: The sacrificial animal conveyed to the sanctuary supplies a context-side image of directed gift and destination.

## thermal transformation
- reading: Exposure also subjects what appears to transforming pressure, making the ban on domination a refusal to turn public light into consuming heat.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Sun exposure and heat supply the thermal field in which prominence can become ordeal.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B004: Food placed on a hot pan until it changes supplies controlled heat as a material transformation.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B002: Meat seized and altered by fire supplies destructive heat as an analogue for overpowering exposed vulnerability.

## lullaby care rhythm
- reading: At the exploratory edge, duha participates in a care rhythm that alternates awakening, settling, sheltering, and soothing rather than forcing immediate motion.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B006: Gentle slowing supplies the unhurried tempo to which the care images attach.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settling stillness supplies the quiet phase of an alternation between waking visibility and rest.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender compassion supplies the relational quality that prevents slowing from becoming neglect.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B006: The non-dominant split image of rocking a child to sleep supplies a rhythmic, soothing mode of guidance.

## voiced daylight
- reading: A live cross-sensory reading lets public brightness become voiced: what is made visible is repeatedly articulated as news.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Public emergence supplies the shared arena in which visible benefit can acquire a voice.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B003: Song and voice convert sufficiency from a silent state into an audible expression.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Renewed speech and news give the audible manifestation recurrence rather than a single utterance.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Making something appear links verbal disclosure back to the focus's visual disclosure.


# Focus 93:2

## settling closure
- reading: An oath by the threshold at which night actively settles, closes in, and quiets a moving field.
- mechanism: Darkness supplies the field while motion in night, sea, wind, and gaze subsides together; the scene closes by becoming still.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night opposed to day supplies the dark temporal field in which the transition occurs.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Still settling across night, water, wind, or gaze supplies the event of motion closing down.

## draped cover
- reading: Night draws itself over the scene like cloth, ambiguously concealing, sheltering, or shrouding it.
- mechanism: Night behaves like a covering drawn across what was visible. The image retains an unresolved range from sheltering cloth to concealing shroud.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's darkness supplies the wide surface-like medium that can cover the visible world.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Draping a cloth, including a shroud, turns nocturnal darkening into a material act of covering.

## night as operation
- reading: Night itself enters as an actor and performs the quieting of the scene.
- mechanism: Night is not only scenery but an operation: it arrives, takes up the field, and performs a settling upon it.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B002: Entering or doing something by night supplies an active nocturnal mode rather than passive clock-time.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settling and closing-in gives that nocturnal mode its operative effect on the surrounding field.

## hidden yield
- reading: Settling may be the quiet surface of a hidden abundance capable of yielding later.
- mechanism: The dark settled surface can be imagined as a reservoir: apparent inactivity may conceal a supply not yet visible.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's opacity makes the quantity held beneath the settled scene unavailable to sight.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B006: Abundant milk or well-water supplies the latent yield hidden inside the apparent stillness.

## day night hinge
- reading: Night's settling is the necessary counterphase of an exposure–repose rhythm whose other edge is daylight's emergence.
- mechanism: The immediately preceding daylight interval extends and emerges into visibility while night settles and closes. The paired oath therefore becomes a hinge between exposure and repose, not an isolated celebration of darkness.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night as day's dark opposite supplies one side of the alternating temporal pair.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settling and closing-in gives the nocturnal side its characteristic movement toward repose.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B001: Daylight's extension supplies a temporal expansion against which nocturnal contraction becomes legible.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sun and visibility supplies the exposed counterphase to night's closing cover.

## quiet without departure
- reading: The silence is a repose held inside continuing care: quiet is not evidence of departure or rejection.
- mechanism: The next ayah places repose beside explicitly negated abandonment and heart-distance, then names a relation of nurturing continuity. Nocturnal quiet is reclassified as held silence rather than withdrawal.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Still settling supplies the silence whose relational meaning is under examination.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B002: Repose and ease overlap with stillness, allowing quiet to be read without severance.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and releasing supplies the feared interpretation that the explicit negation blocks.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repair, and completion supply continuing work beneath the quiet interval.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Abiding continuity prevents the settled night from functioning as a terminal absence.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Heart-distance through aversion supplies a second withdrawal model that the coordinated negation excludes.

## settling with sequel
- reading: The closed scene is an interval with a vector: what is delayed can follow it closely and prove better.
- mechanism: Later, better, beginning, and succession put a vector through the settled scene. Night is no longer a closed endpoint but a delayed interval whose value may appear only in what follows it.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settled closure supplies the apparently terminal state that the later sequence reopens.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Delay to a later time supplies duration beyond the present nocturnal closure.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good assigns positive value to the sequel rather than merely chronological lateness.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply the initial phase from which the settled interval can move.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant mapped image of immediate succession supplies one phase following another without a void.

## cover as incubation
- reading: The cover becomes incubative: hidden time can mature something that will later be handed over and received with satisfaction.
- mechanism: A future handover, nurturing completion, and resulting satisfaction revise the cloth-like cover. What is concealed under night can be undergoing preparation for a gift rather than being sealed away.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: The drawn covering supplies an enclosed interval whose purpose remains initially ambiguous.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handover and giving supply the eventual release of what the interval prepares.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing toward completion supplies active maturation beneath apparent concealment.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance opposed to discontent supplies the affective completion of the concealed process.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B004: The effort to remove another's discontent makes the promised giving purposive and relational.

## cover as shelter
- reading: Night can be the canopy that gathers exposed singularity into shelter, not merely the cloth that hides it.
- mechanism: Finding an isolated one and gathering that one into a merciful shelter selects the protective potential of the focus cover. The shroud-like ambiguity is not erased, but shelter becomes the stronger live direction.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: The cloth-like cover supplies the material form that can either conceal a body or shelter it.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Encountering and locating supplies the first contact with one otherwise exposed or unheld.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Separation from a caretaker supplies the concrete vulnerability that makes covering protective.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Singularity without a counterpart widens orphanhood into the spatial condition of being alone.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Gathering into a shelter supplies the protective destination of the covering motion.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy supplies the affective quality that distinguishes shelter from mere enclosure.

## guidance within opacity
- reading: The suspension can become a guidance chamber: direction is perceived quietly inside opacity before the dark field clears.
- mechanism: Being found in hiddenness or lost direction and then guided gently activates a navigational reading. Nocturnal stillness becomes a low-noise field in which orientation can arrive without first abolishing darkness.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Darkness supplies the opacity in which a route or location is initially unavailable.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: The quieting of motion supplies a receptive field for subtle orientation.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and reaching the obscured one begins the transition from hiddenness to orientation.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Disappearance and hiddenness map the problem onto the night field rather than only moral error.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Missing the intended way supplies the directional problem that guidance resolves.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies orientation without a violent rupture of the scene.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B010: Composed bearing and stillness let guidance resonate with, rather than cancel, the focus quiet.

## quiet reservoir
- reading: Night's stillness can conceal a well of provision whose delayed yield reverses embodied lack.
- mechanism: The sequence from encountered lack and poor nourishment to sufficiency selects the focus verb's abundant-yield branch. Night's still surface becomes reservoir-like: hidden supply answers depletion.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B006: Abundant milk or well-water supplies a reservoir model latent inside the focus verb.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B003: Capacity and material sufficiency link being found to the later reversal of want.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the empty state that makes a hidden yield consequential.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B004: Poor nourishment gives the lack a bodily register answered by milk-like abundance.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Adequacy and sufficiency supply the completed state produced by the reservoir's release.

## noncrushing canopy
- reading: The acceptable cover is explicitly non-crushing: a canopy that protects vulnerability without using its position above to dominate.
- mechanism: A cover laid from above can shelter or press down. The vulnerable orphan and prohibition of domination, sharpened by the grinding-stone image, ethically specify the cover as a canopy that must not become crushing weight.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: The laid cloth supplies an overhead cover whose pressure remains materially ambiguous.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Loss of a caretaker supplies the vulnerable person for whom cover must function as protection.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Domination from above supplies the social force that would convert shelter into subjugation.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B004: A hard grinding stone supplies the material extreme of cover becoming crushing pressure.

## receptive silence
- reading: Settled night models a listening aperture: quiet receives a request and lets need flow without being shouted shut.
- mechanism: A request meets a prohibition of harsh silencing, while the same rebuke-root also carries opening and flowing images. Nocturnal quiet becomes receptive silence: it makes room for need to enter and flow instead of damming speech.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settled quiet supplies the silence whose social function can be either receptive or suppressive.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Question and request supply the voice of need that tests what the quiet permits.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the rejected form of silence imposed by force.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until flow supplies the positive spatial alternative to rebuffing the request.

## concealment to testimony
- reading: The cover is temporary negative space in which care leaves traces that can later be manifested as testimony.
- mechanism: Good condition, abiding nurture, renewed speech, and manifestation turn concealment into a phase of trace-formation. The night-cover hides care temporarily; later telling draws its effects into public visibility.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering supplies the hidden interval whose contents are not yet available to public account.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: A good transformed condition supplies the visible trace left by care during the hidden interval.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease supply the gentle texture of the condition that emerges from concealment.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Abiding continuity supplies care that persists while it is unseen.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as news supplies the later verbal form taken by hidden experience.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something forth into view supplies the reversal from cover to manifestation.

## noncontact restraint
- reading: Night may also settle by refraining from grasp: it leaves protected space around vulnerability and request.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B004: Not touching or taking hold supplies a mode of settling through restraint rather than seizure.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Separation from a caretaker supplies the exposed other whose space must not be seized.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Domination from above supplies the prohibited grasp against which non-contact becomes ethical restraint.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: A voiced request supplies the approach that quiet must receive without possessive taking.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the verbal intrusion excluded by the restraint model.

## hidden tending
- reading: The still exterior may conceal tending: night is a maintenance interval whose work appears only in repaired and sheltered conditions.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B005: Working and tending an estate supplies active maintenance concealed beneath the settled exterior.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion supply the kind of work the hidden interval can perform.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Abiding persistence supplies continuity between unseen tending and later visible effects.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Gathering into shelter supplies one concrete outcome of custodial work.

## lullaby stilling
- reading: Night can resonate as a vast calming motion, rocking exposed life into a sheltered stillness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Still settling supplies the bodily and environmental quiet produced by calming.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Loss of a caretaker supplies the exposed child-scale condition that makes calming poignant.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy supplies the affective quality of the calming motion.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B006: The non-dominant mapped image of rocking a child to sleep supplies the lullaby mechanism.


# Focus 93:3

## dual rupture denied
- reading: The statement denies both external withdrawal of care and internal expulsion from regard: interruption is neither abandonment nor aversion.
- mechanism: The focus denies two distinct rupture mechanisms: outward release from care and inward aversion. The nurture-and-completion branch of رب makes the relation actively maintained rather than merely not terminated.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and letting go supplies the outward rupture blocked by the first negation.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and stagewise completion makes the intervening Rabb an agent of continuing care.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Heart-distancing aversion supplies the inward rupture blocked by the second negation.

## no departure or casting out
- reading: No farewell, departure, distancing, or casting outside has occurred; the perceived gap does not relocate the addressee beyond the Rabb's continuing relation.
- mechanism: A farewell initiates departure, light rising separates a body from its place, and driving casts it outside. Negating these around a Rabb characterized by staying produces a spatial model of unbroken proximity.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B003: Farewell at departure supplies the leave-taking event that the first ما cancels.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Staying, clinging, and lasting supplies the relation's counterforce to departure.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B001: Quick movement held apart from a place supplies an image of apparent spatial separation.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Driving or casting outside supplies the terminal expulsion that the second ما denies.

## non delegated guardianship
- reading: The addressee has not been handed off beyond the caregiver's responsibility or treated as a charge that can be expelled.
- mechanism: A deposit can be handed to another custodian, while a foster relation names responsibility for raising one placed in care. Under the paired negation, the addressee is neither transferred out as a deposited object nor subsequently cast away.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B005: Entrusting something as a deposit supplies the possible transfer of custody negated in the first clause.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The fostered-child and caregiver relation turns Rabb into a concrete guardianship role.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Casting outside supplies the feared disposal of one whose custody has become burdensome.

## growth continuity
- reading: The focus also carries an exploratory denial that an unfinished work of nourishment and growth has been interrupted or thrown out.
- mechanism: If Rabb activates nourishment and growing up rather than only title or ownership, abandonment and expulsion become ways of interrupting a developmental process. Their negation preserves a trajectory still being brought toward maturity.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and neglecting supplies the interruption that would stop an unfinished process.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant mapped branch of nourishment and growing up recasts Rabb as developmental care.
