  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Driving or casting out supplies premature removal from the field in which growth occurs.

## pause as cyclic cover
- reading: Silence can be a settled, covering phase whose hidden continuity will reappear; nonmanifest care is not abandoned care.
- mechanism: The pre-focus oaths stage bright emergence followed by darkness that settles and covers. Coupled with ودع's calm-rest branch, an interval of nonmanifestation becomes a containing phase inside a cycle rather than evidence of severance.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B002: Restful ease and settled calm supplies a pause-state that need not equal abandonment.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Aversion names the feared emotional explanation that the cyclic model displaces.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight and visibility supplies one pole of the cycle.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night and its darkness supplies the contrasting interval of nonvisibility.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Stillness with night or sea settling supplies a stable pause rather than a departing agent.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth makes concealment compatible with continued presence underneath.

## delay not rejection
- reading: Non-abandonment can include a delay whose later issue is better: deferred care is temporally discontinuous in appearance but not relationally severed.
- mechanism: Laterness, benefit, return to an outcome, and succession introduce ordered time between an earlier condition and what follows. The focus negations therefore distinguish deferred unfolding from rejection or expulsion.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving supplies the false conclusion drawn when delay is mistaken for termination.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Casting outside supplies the rejected model of what the interval means.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time makes delay an explicit temporal mechanism.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good gives the deferred phase a positive direction.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to a thing's outcome makes the first phase intelligible from its eventual issue.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant mapped image of one thing following another supplies continuity across distinct stages.

## care as giving toward satisfaction
- reading: The focus protects an unfinished trajectory: the Rabb remains engaged by giving and nurturing toward a satisfaction not yet reached.
- mechanism: Future giving by the repeated Rabb and its movement toward satisfaction converts negative reassurance into a forward-moving process. Care does not merely remain present; it transfers benefit and carries an unfinished relation toward fulfillment.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Negated leaving supplies the continuity that future giving positively demonstrates.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: Nourishment and growing up supplies the developmental baseline strengthened by the promise.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handing over and giving supplies an observable act of continuing care.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion identifies the giver's action as sustained cultivation.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B002: Abundant or sought satisfaction supplies the process's recipient-centered endpoint.

## orphaned state to active guardianship
- reading: The Rabb who encountered one cut off from a guardian gathered him into care; non-abandonment now means no lapse of guardianship even when shelter is mediated through a created refuge.
- mechanism: Being cut off from a caretaker is answered by being encountered, gathered into shelter, and treated with mercy. This converts the focus's caregiver and custody branches from lexical possibilities into a remembered mechanism of active guardianship.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B005: Deposit and custody supplies the question of who continues to hold responsibility for the addressee.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The fostered-child and caregiver relation supplies a concrete model of guardianship.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Encountering and finding supplies active attention to the vulnerable state.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child cut off from a protector supplies the precise social shape of threatened abandonment.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining someone to a gathering refuge supplies the spatial reversal of being cut off.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy toward one in need supplies the affective reversal of aversion.

## continuously findable and redirected
- reading: Not being abandoned means remaining findable within every obscured or needy condition and repeatedly receiving the response that condition requires.
- mechanism: The repeated pattern of finding the addressee in a deficit and then reversing it turns non-abandonment into operational attention: hiddenness is met by gentle direction, and need by sufficiency. The addressee remains findable and actionable even when his condition looks like loss.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and neglect supplies the condition contradicted by repeated finding and response.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Casting outside supplies the feared state of being beyond recovery or response.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding or coming upon the addressee supplies attentive contact before correction.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Disappearance and hiddenness supplies a non-moralized form of seeming absence.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies restorative orientation rather than coercive retrieval.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: The repeated act of finding makes attention a pattern rather than a single rescue.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supplies a second state in which abandonment could be inferred.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the concrete reversal of unattended need.

## received nonabandonment becomes social rule
- reading: The focus becomes a social prototype: one assured of not being abandoned must refuse to make an orphan or petitioner experience abandonment through power or speech.
- mechanism: The one cut off from a protector and the one who asks are paired with prohibitions against domination and harsh verbal repulsion. The focus's negated abandonment and casting-away become an imitable ethic: received care must not be inverted upon another vulnerable person.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and neglect supplies the relational harm that the later social commands prevent.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Driving or casting outside supplies the action-level counterpart of repelling a vulnerable person.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The child cut off from a protector transfers the addressee's remembered vulnerability to another recipient.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Dominating from above and humiliating supplies a social way to enact abandonment while remaining physically present.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking and seeking supplies a second exposed dependency that calls for response.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the interpersonal expulsion prohibited in answer to need.

## nonabandonment made new in telling
- reading: The denial acquires a testimonial life: remembered effects of the Rabb's care are repeatedly made new and visible in speech.
- mechanism: Good condition and favor are joined to the repeated Rabb and to speech that renews news and makes something manifest. Non-abandonment is therefore not only remembered inwardly; its traces are kept present through renewed telling.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion supplies the sustained care whose effects can be narrated.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Negated leaving supplies the claim for which remembered favors become visible evidence.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and favor supplies the experienced material to be voiced.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: The repeated nurturing Rabb links the closing imperative back to the focus relation.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as news supplies repetition that keeps prior care newly audible.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Showing and making manifest turns hidden continuity into communicable evidence.

## not used up in need
- reading: At an exploratory social-material register, he was not put into expendable service and worn through need; nurturing care moves dependence toward sufficiency.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B009: Using someone in one's need or wearing a protected thing in service supplies an image of expendability.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion supplies the opposite relation of restoring rather than consuming capacity.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supplies the pressure under which a dependent person could be treated as expendable.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the restorative outcome opposed to being used up.

## rebuff produces hidden withdrawal
- reading: The later rule prevents a human agent from causing the hidden withdrawal that the focus denies: do not make the asker slip away as one cast out.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Letting go supplies the relational failure that allows a vulnerable person to disappear.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Driving outside supplies the force that can turn a request into withdrawal.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking and seeking grounds the actual social role before the split-root activation is considered.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B004: The non-dominant image of slipping away supplies the requester's possible hidden response to rejection.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the inferred cause of that quiet withdrawal.

## not cast onto transforming heat
- reading: At a contained material register, the negation also resists imagining the addressee as something cast onto heat to be drained and forcibly altered rather than nurtured.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B004: Putting food onto a hot pan until it changes supplies the focus-side material image.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B002: Flesh seized by fire, drained, and altered supplies the context-side transformation mechanism.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing completion contrasts formative care with coercive alteration by heat.


# Focus 93:4

## temporal benefit gradient
- reading: The later phase is beneficially superior for the addressee to the initial phase.
- mechanism: A later phase follows an initial phase and is evaluated not merely as later but as more beneficial for the addressee.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness supplies the second position in a sequence and makes it the positively ranked horizon.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Desired benefit supplies the comparison's value axis, making superiority practical rather than merely chronological.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply the initial condition that the later condition exceeds.

## deferred outcome
- reading: The delay itself can carry the first condition toward a better outcome whose value is not yet visible.
- mechanism: What is deferred can acquire its proper valuation only at its outcome, so the initial condition cannot settle the comparison.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time makes delay an active interval rather than an empty gap.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B003: Seeking or choosing the better option makes the comparison a movement toward an outcome, not a static label.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to final outcome or meaning supplies the point at which the deferred good becomes intelligible.

## beneficent allotment
- reading: The later bears a beneficent allotment addressed specifically to you.
- mechanism: The comparison concerns an allotted good: the later carries generosity specifically toward the addressee rather than an impersonal ranking.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The later member locates the beneficent allotment beyond the initial phase.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: Beneficence and gift turn khayr into directed giving and make laka function as its recipient relation.

## successive priority
- reading: Within continuous succession, the later horizon has the better claim on your confidence and orientation.
- mechanism: The two terms can be heard as adjacent claims within an unbroken succession; the later horizon becomes the worthier claimant on the addressee's orientation.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The other or later member supplies the horizon that follows the presently prior one.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B003: Choosing the better option makes rival temporal claims solicit orientation.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Continuous succession keeps first and later contiguous rather than sealing them into unrelated domains.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B008: Priority and superior right recast the comparison as a contest over which horizon deserves precedence.

## covered interval not abandonment
- reading: The covered present is a quiet interval within continuous care; its latent good becomes visible later without implying prior abandonment.
- mechanism: Manifest daylight gives way to a settled covering, while the paired negations reject both severance and affective distance and abiding lordship holds the interval together. The later good is therefore latent during concealment, not created after an abandonment.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral supplies a later disclosure and lets the intervening interval carry rather than cancel the promise.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good identifies what remains real even before it becomes manifest.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight supplies the visible pole against which hidden good can later appear.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night and its darkness supply a phase of nonvisibility without themselves implying loss.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settled stillness makes the dark interval a composed state rather than evidence of rupture.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering by a cloth supplies the image of something present yet temporarily concealed.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and relinquishment name the severance that the clause explicitly negates.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Abiding and duration supply continuity across the apparently silent interval.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Heart-distancing aversion names the affective withdrawal that the second negation excludes.

## cultivated completion
- reading: The better later phase is the cultivated completion and growth of a first phase that was never left untended.
- mechanism: Repeated lordship activates repair, upbringing, completion, nourishment, and growth. Later goodness develops from a continuously tended first condition instead of merely replacing it.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness supplies the developed phase reached after tending.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B002: Chosen excellence supplies the qualitative improvement produced by development.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: The beginning supplies the seed condition from which completion proceeds.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, upbringing, and completion supply the process that joins first condition to better later condition.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant nourishment-and-growth image makes improvement organic and cumulative.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Abiding duration holds cultivation continuous through promise, reception, and later report.

## recipient calibrated gift
- reading: Better is a personally delivered good whose adequacy is registered in the addressee's satisfaction.
- mechanism: The future is enacted as a handover whose sequel is acceptance or satisfaction. Khayr becomes a gift whose goodness is calibrated at the recipient rather than an abstract quantity.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: Beneficence and gift supply the focus comparison's transferable good.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handover and giving make the promised better concrete as something delivered to the addressee.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction opposed to displeasure supplies the recipient-side test of the gift's goodness.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Mutual acceptance keeps the promised benefit relational rather than reducible to sheer accumulation.

## serial state transitions
- reading: The focus also names a recurrent grammar of care: a vulnerable first state is found and actively carried into a concretely better next state.
- mechanism: Three repeated findings expose severance, lostness, and need, and each is followed by a concrete transition into shelter, guidance, or sufficiency. The focus comparison becomes a reusable transformation pattern rather than only one distant binary.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The later member supplies the transformed state on the far side of each intervention.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good evaluates each resulting state as a real improvement.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply each vulnerable starting state.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and encountering initiate the first intervention rather than leaving vulnerability unseen.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caretaker supplies the first vulnerable state.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering shelter supplies the first better state.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Repeated finding restarts the transition mechanism for a second domain.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Being away from guidance and intended direction supplies the second starting state.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward path and truth supplies the second transition.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: A third finding confirms that noticed need repeatedly becomes the entry point of change.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Want and poverty supply the third starting state.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the third better state without requiring limitless wealth.

## guardianship and better claim
- reading: The later relation is better entitled and better fitted to stand near you and take charge after an earlier severance.
- mechanism: The split W-L-Y inventory in the focus activates charge, proximity, and superior right; orphaned severance followed by shelter then lets the later horizon be imagined as a relation better entitled and better able to take charge.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B003: Assuming charge and guardianship turns the focus comparison toward who or what tends the addressee's affair.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B008: Superior right lets the later horizon be heard as the worthier claimant to that charge.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B002: Chosen excellence qualifies the later relation as better fitted, not merely subsequent.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caretaker supplies the relational lack to which guardianship answers.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a sheltering side supplies the restored relation of proximity and care.

## hidden appearance to meaning
- reading: The later construal or outcome can be better for you than the first visible appearance of events.
- mechanism: Hiddenness and gentle indication activate a movement from an initially visible outline toward a later outcome or final meaning. The temporal comparison acquires an epistemic layer: first appearance is not final interpretation.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The later member supplies the subsequently disclosed interpretive horizon.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B006: A visible outline or shimmering appearance supplies the provisional first impression.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome or final meaning supplies the deeper reading reached beyond appearance.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness supplies why the better meaning may be unavailable at first sight.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication supplies the process by which hidden outcome becomes legible.

## sufficiency not accumulation
- reading: A better future can mean the decisive arrival of enough: benefit measured by freedom from need, not by limitless accumulation.
- mechanism: The explicit passage from want to sufficiency constrains the value axis of khayr. Better need not mean maximal accumulation; it can mean release from disabling dependence into enough.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good lets adequacy, rather than sheer amount, define what counts as better.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Want and poverty establish the disabling threshold from which improvement is measured.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Adequacy and sufficiency supply a bounded yet genuine better state.

## benefit as ethical relay
- reading: The later good becomes an ethical relay: its beneficiary must not close another vulnerable person's path toward a better next state.
- mechanism: The beneficiary of promised khayr is forbidden to impose downward domination or harsh verbal blockage on vulnerable others. The private later good becomes transmissive: its truth is enacted by refusing to make another person's next state worse.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: Beneficence and gift make the focus good capable of being relayed beyond its first recipient.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caretaker identifies a person whose next state is especially exposed to the beneficiary's conduct.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Domination from above names the social mechanism that would reproduce an inferior state and is prohibited.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Request and seeking identify another vulnerable approach to the recipient of good.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke names the blockage that would stop beneficence at the recipient and is prohibited.

## narrated renewal
- reading: The better later state remains generative: received as favor, it is disclosed and made newly present through narration.
- mechanism: Good condition is named as a bestowed favor, then made into renewed and disclosed speech. The better later state is not a silent terminus; once narrated, it becomes a fresh event that can reactivate benefit.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness supplies the newly arrived state that can subsequently be reported.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: Gift and beneficence identify what the later state carries and what narration passes onward.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and favor identify the received better as grace rather than self-generated possession.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair and completion attribute the favor to an ongoing tending agency.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as news turns received good into an event that can recur in telling.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Disclosure and manifestation reverse the earlier possibility of covered or hidden good.

## alternate exit
- reading: Closure of the first route can be the means by which hidden life is gently redirected toward a better later opening.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B006: Coaxing an animal from a burrow by obstructing one opening supplies an indirect route into the better.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering supplies the enclosed or unavailable first route.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness supplies the unseen position from which emergence must occur.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction supplies the rerouting toward a viable opening rather than forced extraction.

## late rain ecology
- reading: As an ecological analogy, the later can be the follow-up rain whose added watering turns hidden saturation into flowing, usable benefit.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness supplies the second watering in the ecological sequence.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Benefit supplies the fertility or usefulness by which the later watering is judged.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B010: Rain following the wasmi supplies a concrete image of a later gift arriving after an initial rain.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B006: Abundance in milk or well-water supplies stored saturation before outward flow.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B008: Rain cloud supplies the carrier from which the later watering descends.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B001: A river cutting earth with running water supplies the later visible distribution of accumulated benefit.

## rear over front
- reading: The ayah reverses visible rank: what has been placed behind may carry greater worth than what forces itself to the front or above.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B003: The posterior or rear supplies what ordinary hierarchy leaves behind or out of view.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Precedence supplies the socially visible front position against which the rear is reevaluated.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B002: Chosen excellence denies that front position by itself determines worth.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Domination from above supplies the hierarchy whose ranking of front and rear is ethically interrupted.


# Focus 93:5

## nurtured satisfaction
- reading: The Lord will keep administering a need-responsive gift until it reaches completion in the recipient's own acceptance.
- mechanism: Giving is not exhausted by transfer. The Lord's branch of stagewise repair and nurture makes the gift an administered process, while the service branch makes it responsive to the recipient's need; satisfaction is the recipient-side completion condition.
- trace:
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B003: Attending to a person and handing over what is wanted makes the transfer responsive rather than generic.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion stage by stage supply the process that shapes the giving.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance rather than displeasure supplies the recipient-side endpoint of the process.

## consent completed handover
- reading: Authority hands something over in a relation whose declared endpoint includes the receiver's capacity to consent to and accept it.
- mechanism: A handing-over by one who has authority could be unilateral, but the mutual-consent branch of satisfaction keeps the receiver from being merely passive. The event is relationally complete when what is handed over can be accepted.
- trace:
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Concrete handing over supplies a transfer whose completion can remain open until reception.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and authority establish the asymmetry that the recipient's response unexpectedly qualifies.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Mutual consent contributes an active receiving relation rather than imposed contentment.

## growing horizon
- reading: The promise opens a growing horizon: cultivated increases enlarge what the recipient can receive until abundant approval becomes possible.
- mechanism: The packet's non-dominant mapping of the Lord-root activates increase and growing-up alongside the dominant nurture branch. Joined to abundant or specially sought approval, this makes the promise a cultivated expansion of capacity rather than one fixed item.
- trace:
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Giving supplies the recurring material event through which growth can be realized.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B001: The non-dominant increase-and-rising image opens an expanding rather than static scale.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: Nourishing and growing up turns increase into cultivated development rather than mere addition.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B002: Abundant or specially sought approval provides an enlarged horizon toward which the giving grows.

## dawned delay
- reading: The delay can be a gentle phase of emergence in which nurtured provision moves from latent to visible.
- mechanism: Emergence into visibility and gentle delay give the focus's futurity a dawn-like mechanism. The promised transfer can be paced disclosure: what is not yet handed over is moving toward manifestation under patient nurture.
- trace:
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Coming out into the sun and becoming visible models future giving as emergence from latency.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B006: Gentleness and granting time keep delay from functioning as refusal.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Stagewise nurture supplies the agency that paces the emergence.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: The future handover remains the focus event to which the temporal image attaches.

## covered continuity
- reading: The promise can include a covered interval in which care persists quietly before its visible handover.
- mechanism: Darkness, settling stillness, and covering distinguish invisibility from cessation. Joined to the Lord-root's persistence, they allow giving to remain active under a quiet cover before it becomes a handover.
- trace:
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's darkness supplies the interval in which provision is not perceptible.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settling stillness makes the hidden interval quiet without making it empty.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering supplies a material distinction between concealed activity and terminated activity.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Staying and lasting carry the giver's relation through the covered interval.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handover is recast as the visible phase of a provision that may already persist.

## unbroken care
- reading: The future handover manifests care that remained attached throughout the apparently quiet interval.
- mechanism: The doubled negation blocks both departure and affective distancing, while the repeated Lord-root contributes lasting nurture. The future gift therefore reads less like compensation after abandonment and more like a visible phase of an unbroken relation.
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and relinquishing identify the severance explicitly placed under negation.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Staying, clinging, and lasting provide the positive relational counterimage to the negated departure.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Hatred that distances the heart identifies a second kind of separation explicitly negated.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Ongoing nurture turns relational continuity into the mechanism of the promised giving.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: The handover becomes evidence of continuity rather than a restart after rupture.

## later outcome
- reading: Successive, administered goods move toward a later outcome from which earlier stages are revalued, with acceptance as the horizon.
- mechanism: Laterness, beneficial good, eventual outcome, and close succession turn the promise into an ordered trajectory. Satisfaction is not merely an emotion after one transfer; it is the horizon from which a sequence of administered stages becomes intelligibly better.
- trace:
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Being later than what is first establishes directional asymmetry across stages.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time makes delay part of the temporal mechanism rather than an empty gap.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward what is beneficial gives the later direction a qualitative measure.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: Generosity and gift directly connect the better-later comparison to the focus's giving.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an eventual outcome makes the end capable of reinterpreting the preceding stages.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant image of one thing following another sharpens the promise into close succession.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Stagewise administration supplies an agent for ordering the successive goods.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance functions as the horizon at which the ordered trajectory becomes complete.

## shelter shaped gift
- reading: The promised giving can restore and deepen belonging, attending to a history of severed support until the recipient feels securely received.
- mechanism: Encountering a person cut off from a caregiver is followed by gathering, shelter, and mercy. That past state-to-intervention pattern activates the focus Lord as caregiver and the gift as attended restoration of belonging; satisfaction can be secure attachment rather than quantity.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Finding or coming upon supplies the moment at which a vulnerable state becomes attended.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child's severance from a caregiver identifies the relational lack to be repaired.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining someone into a gathering or shelter supplies the concrete repair of severed support.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy specifies the affective quality of the sheltering intervention.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The fostered-child and caregiver relation makes the focus Lord a direct anchor for restored belonging.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B003: Attending to a person's need recasts giving as caregiving rather than impersonal transfer.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance marks the felt completion of restored security.

## guidance as gift
- reading: Giving may furnish orientation itself, gently making a path findable and acceptable to the recipient.
- mechanism: Being found hidden, missing, or without direction is answered by gentle indication; the guidance-root also carries a gift sent in affection. This makes the focus's giving capable of being orientation itself: a gift that renders a path receivable and meaningful.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies the transition from an unlocated state into attended relation.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness makes the problem one of unavailable orientation, not simply moral failure.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B003: Loss supplies the spatial and relational condition that guidance reverses.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the non-coercive form of the intervention.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B004: A kindness or gift dispatched to one loved directly bridges guidance and giving.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handover anchors the guidance image back in the focus's explicit act of giving.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance becomes recognition that the offered orientation fits the recipient.

## enoughness boundary
- reading: The gift is calibrated to restore sufficient capacity, and satisfaction names an enoughness boundary rather than an appetite for endless increase.
- mechanism: The movement from poverty or incapacitating lack to sufficiency supplies a calibration principle for the indefinite gift. Satisfaction can mark enoughness—the point where provision restores capacity—rather than limitless accumulation.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B003: Capacity, means, and wealth place the found state on a material axis.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need identify the deficit from which the intervention begins.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B009: Something proving unmanageable widens lack into impaired agency, not merely low quantity.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies a bounded target in which need is answered and agency restored.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B003: Need-attending service makes the amount and form of giving responsive to the actual deficit.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the experiential boundary at which responsive provision becomes enough.

## anti coercive power
- reading: The promise models power that serves without crushing; the recipient's satisfaction marks preserved agency rather than submission.
- mechanism: The context prohibits domination from above toward one already severed from protection. Set beside lordly authority and mutual satisfaction, this changes the geometry of the promise: power bends into service, and the recipient's satisfaction acts as an anti-humiliation constraint.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caregiver identifies the person for whom vertical power is especially dangerous.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Overpowering from above and humiliating the overpowered supplies the relation explicitly prohibited.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and authority supply the vertical power that can be exercised without humiliation.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B003: Service and attending to need provide the non-dominating mode of powerful giving.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Mutual consent keeps the recipient's agency visible inside an asymmetrical relation.

## requester reversal
- reading: Receiving without humiliation trains the satisfied recipient to remain open to petitioners rather than closing around possession.
- mechanism: The giving-root itself contains a branch of asking people for a gift; the later requester and prohibition of harsh rebuke activate that latent role reversal. The promised receiver can remain structurally continuous with future askers, so satisfaction need not become social closure.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Questioning and requesting make need approach the now-addressed recipient from the other side.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the response explicitly withheld from the requester.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B005: The focus root's asking-for-a-gift branch lets giver and petitioner remain reversible positions.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handover supplies the positive response whose social memory should survive being a receiver.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Relational consent prevents satisfaction from being reduced to private possession.

## open channel
- reading: Giving can open a managed channel of provision, and satisfaction need not function as a dam against later requests.
- mechanism: A cross-branch activation within the prohibited rebuke-root supplies a river cut through earth and an opening widened until flow becomes possible. In the presence of a requester, this converts the one-time handover into an image of provision whose channel should not be closed by satisfaction.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The request supplies pressure at the edge of the recipient's newly acquired provision.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B001: A river cleaving ground with running water supplies the unexpected image of an enduring channel.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until something flows supplies the channel-forming mechanism.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: The explicit handover anchors the flow image in the promised act of giving.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Administration and nurture keep flow responsive rather than indiscriminate.

## reportable gift
- reading: Satisfaction is also the threshold at which nurtured favor becomes recognizable, disclosable, and newly tellable.
- mechanism: Good condition and eye-delighting favor are joined to the same Lord relation and then to renewed speech and disclosure. Satisfaction therefore becomes a threshold of articulation: provision is completed socially when it can be recognized, made present in words, and renewed as news.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: A good condition and favor identify the received state as more than an isolated object.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B013: The eye-delighting idiom gives satisfaction an embodied affective register.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repeated nurture links the narrated favor to the same continuing source as the focus promise.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as report turns received favor into an event that can remain newly tellable.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something out and showing it opposes private concealment of the received good.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: The handover remains the originating event that becomes reportable.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance marks the point from which the gift can be acknowledged without denying its process.

## pliant circumstances
- reading: The Lord may give by patiently making resistant circumstances yield into a form the recipient can inhabit with acceptance.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B006: Pliant yielding supplies an image of resistant circumstances becoming workable for the recipient.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and repair provide the agentive process that can make circumstances workable.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance marks the point at which the reshaped circumstances become livable.

## displeasure outdone
- reading: Provision keeps outdoing residual deprivation until displeasure loses its hold, without turning the recipient into the defeated party.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B007: Outdoing another in mutual dealing supplies the first half of a contest against persisting lack.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B005: Overcoming in a matter supplies the reversal in which dissatisfaction, rather than the recipient, loses.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Authority locates the capacity to sustain the giving until residual lack is outdone.

## entrusted covenant
- reading: The future gift may be imagined as a securely held trust under covenant, awaiting a handover that completes in acceptance.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B005: Deposit and entrustment supply an image of something preserved during the interval before handover.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B011: A binding covenant supplies assurance that the held provision is relationally secured.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Future handing over supplies the delivery phase of the trust image.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance marks release of the entrusted good into completed reception.

## sheltering lullaby
- reading: One live affective resonance is that caregiving provision settles a formerly unprotected recipient into secure, accepting repose.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settling stillness supplies the quiet bodily environment of repose.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caregiver supplies the vulnerability for which secure rest matters.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy supplies the affective quality of shelter.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B006: The non-dominant lullaby image supplies a highly contained mechanism of soothing into rest.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The caregiver relation anchors the soothing image directly in the focus's giver.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance becomes the felt settling of a once-unprotected recipient.


# Focus 93:6

## protective rehousing
- reading: A compact repair sequence: exposed dependency is recognized and answered by incorporation into a protective locus.
- mechanism: Finding identifies the vulnerable person, orphanhood specifies severance from a protecting parent, and the consequential فَ turns recognition into drawing that person toward a gathering refuge.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Coming upon or perceptually apprehending supplies the recognition that initiates the response.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Being cut off from a protecting parent defines the precise vulnerability that needs repair.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Drawing another into a sheltering or gathering point supplies the fitted counter-movement to protective severance.

## solitary to gathered
- reading: Orphanhood is also a topology of aloneness, and shelter changes that topology from solitary exposure to gathered belonging.
- mechanism: The peerless or solitary branch of orphanhood and the gathering branch of shelter form a social-spatial reversal: an isolated node is brought into a place of attachment.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Finding makes the isolated condition perceptible rather than leaving it socially unseen.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Isolated singleness supplies the relational topology of one without a peer or surrounding bond.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Gathering into a refuge reverses isolated singleness by giving it a point of belonging.

## compassionate recognition
- reading: The finding can carry felt recognition of orphaned loss, with shelter as compassion made operative.
- mechanism: The inward sorrow-or-love branch of finding and the pity branch of shelter make the encounter more than detection: felt recognition becomes compassionate turning toward the orphaned person.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B004: Inner sorrow or love gives the act of finding an affective resonance, while not replacing the finite verb's ordinary syntax.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Loss of the protecting parent is the condition toward which concern is directed.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tenderness and pity convert inward recognition into compassionate action.

## delayed care reversed
- reading: It also stages a temporal reversal: care that characteristically lags behind the orphan is made to arrive as an immediate compassionate response.
- mechanism: A branch of orphanhood images kindness as arriving late; the verse reverses that lag by making recognition issue directly into compassionate refuge.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Recognition marks the point at which an unattended condition becomes actively encountered.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B004: Slowness or delayed beneficence supplies a temporal dimension to orphaned vulnerability.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Compassionate turning is the action that interrupts the delay in care.

## visibility and cover
- reading: Shelter is a stable relation capable of both bringing the isolated person into view and covering that person through periods of darkness or stillness.
- mechanism: The opening alternation supplies both emergence into light and enveloping stillness. Applied back to the focus, refuge need not mean uninterrupted exposure or uninterrupted concealment; it can preserve the formerly isolated person through a rhythm of being seen and being covered.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Solitary isolation is the focus-state whose continued presence must be secured.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: A gathering refuge supplies the stable protective center across changing conditions.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into the sun contributes the phase of becoming visible rather than remaining socially hidden.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's darkness contributes the contrasting condition in which protection cannot depend on visibility.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth turns darkness into an image of protective enclosure rather than simple absence.

## ongoing custody
- reading: The sheltering manifests an enduring guardianship marked by non-relinquishment, continued formation, and refusal of affective distance.
- mechanism: The double negation rejects both relinquishment and affective distancing, while lordship contributes nurture and completion. The past sheltering event therefore becomes evidence of a custody that continues rather than an isolated rescue that has expired.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Loss of a protector establishes the relational breach to which continuing custody answers.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Being drawn into refuge supplies the initial act of protective custody.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and releasing, under negation, rules out the severance of the sheltering relation.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and bringing to completion gives the protecting subject an ongoing developmental role.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Hatred that distances the heart, also under negation, excludes affective withdrawal as well as physical abandonment.

## shelter as inaugural care
- reading: Shelter opens a sustained pedagogy of care whose later phases continue giving and formation toward satisfaction.
- mechanism: The later/first comparison and the promise of giving toward satisfaction place the focus rescue on a longer developmental arc. Shelter is the inaugural fitted gift, not the ceiling of care.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: The sheltering act supplies the concrete first stage from which the longer arc can unfold.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness establishes a horizon beyond the already completed rescue.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good makes the temporal horizon an improving trajectory rather than mere succession.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence cast the earlier condition as an initial phase rather than a final measure.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Giving by conveyance supplies the continuing movement of care toward the addressee.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion make the successive gifts stages in formation rather than disconnected favors.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction supplies the prospective human terminus toward which the care moves.

## matched remedy triad
- reading: It is the first unit in a triad of matched repairs: shelter answers lost protection and belonging specifically, rather than standing for guidance or wealth in general.
- mechanism: The two following clauses repeat the found-state-then-response syntax but change both the diagnosed lack and its remedy. This parallelism differentiates three domains: severed protection receives refuge, lost direction receives guidance, and material dependency receives sufficiency.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies the repeated diagnostic operation shared by all three clauses.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Loss of a protecting parent specifies the first clause's relational vulnerability.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Gathering into refuge is the remedy fitted specifically to relational and spatial exposure.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: The repeated finding verb confirms a common recognition-response template.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness or disappearance supplies a distinct directional or epistemic lack.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the second clause's fitted remedy.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: A third recurrence preserves the same diagnostic hinge while changing domains again.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need define the economic lack rather than repeating orphanhood.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the economic remedy and keeps it distinct from refuge or guidance.

## received refuge becomes ethic
- reading: The sheltering becomes an ethical relay: remembered protection forbids reproducing vertical pressure against another orphan.
- mechanism: The exact orphan root returns as the object of a prohibition against domination from above. The received act of refuge is thereby relayed into an ethic: one who was gathered under protection must not use superior position to humiliate another who bears the same severance.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: The addressee's own loss of a protector supplies the remembered vulnerability.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Compassionate turning supplies the positive treatment received in the focus.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The repeated orphan category transfers the focus vulnerability from remembered self to present other.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Dominating from above and abasing the weaker party, under prohibition, defines the conduct opposite to sheltering compassion.

## refuge with open threshold
- reading: Refuge can become a hospitable social threshold: it protects without turning its boundary into a rebuke that drives petitioners away.
- mechanism: The asker brings a request to a social boundary; harsh rebuke would close that boundary, while the same root inventory also supplies widening until flow. Under the prohibition, the focus refuge activates as a permeable shelter whose protection does not harden into exclusion.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: A gathering point supplies the focus image of a place that can receive rather than repel.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Questioning and requesting place a petitioner at the boundary of another's attention and resources.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke, under negation, identifies the boundary-closing response that is forbidden.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until something can flow supplies a latent counter-image for how the social threshold might remain passable.

## refuge becomes testimony
- reading: Shelter also restores social voice: the gathered recipient can renew and disclose the memory of care as testimony.
- mechanism: Good condition and nurturing lordship are followed by renewed, revealing speech. The once-private sheltering event can therefore mature into narrated grace: refuge not only gives a place but restores a position from which the recipient can speak.
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Solitary isolation supplies the silent or socially detached starting condition.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Gathering into refuge supplies the relational placement from which testimony becomes possible.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Blessing and good condition classify the sheltering reversal as a beneficent state worth acknowledging.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion connect the remembered rescue to the same continuing source of formation.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as report turns the sheltered past into a repeatedly voiced account.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something forth and making it visible supplies the publicizing movement of testimony.

## severed edges drawn together
- reading: As a contained material analogy, the line resembles cut protective edges being drawn back together inside a healing enclosure.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B003: The split target's cutting and severing image supplies a material shadow for the broken protective relation.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Being cut off from a protecting parent anchors the severing image in the explicit focus predicate.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Drawing into a gathering point, whose scope also includes wound edges drawing together, supplies the counter-image of repair.

## cradle like settling
- reading: As a carefully qualified cross-branch resonance, shelter also feels cradle-like: the vulnerable dependent is gathered and gently settled.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: The child cut off from parental protection anchors the dependent figure in the focus.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Drawing the dependent into refuge supplies the enclosing movement.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: The repeated finding verb links the context response structurally back to the focus response.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B006: The split target's image of gently rocking a child to sleep contributes a settling motion to the sheltering scene.

## guardianship taken up
- reading: In a contained split-root reading, the vacant work of protection is actively taken up as supportive guardianship whose first act is shelter.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: The absent protecting parent creates the vacant guardianship role.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Giving refuge supplies the concrete act by which protection is assumed.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B003: The split target's taking charge of an affair sharpens shelter into active guardianship.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B004: The split target's love, support, and alliance prevent that guardianship from becoming merely administrative custody.


# Focus 93:7

## course correction
- reading: A compact event-chain: encounter exposes a missed course, and guidance answers by gently making a viable way available.
- mechanism: Finding discloses the addressee as missing the intended course; the fa-transition makes gentle route-giving the fitting response rather than leaving ضَالًّا as a static label.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and coming upon supplies the encounter that reveals the condition.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Departure from guidance or aim defines the condition functionally as being off course.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a way or truth supplies the corrective motion.

## recovery from hiddenness
- reading: It can also name an obscured or unmanifest condition from which encounter and a visible lead bring the addressee forth.
- mechanism: The addressee is not only off route but obscured from view or absorbed into an undifferentiated field; being found reverses concealment, and guidance places a leading edge before him.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Coming upon what was not evident provides the reversal from concealment to encounter.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness and disappearance recast the predicate as obscurity rather than only wrong direction.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B003: The leading front part gives the newly visible subject something ahead to follow.

## lost one given a course
- reading: Finding is recovery, while guiding is the second, nonredundant act of giving the recovered one a course.
- mechanism: The grammar permits a recovery model: a lost one is located and then not merely possessed but assigned a course, converting recovery into purposive movement.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding a sought or lost thing supplies the recovery event.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B003: Loss or failure to locate supplies the recoverable condition.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course, manner, and intended direction turn recovery into an oriented life-path.

## supported passage
- reading: Guidance may accompany and steady the off-course traveler until movement itself acquires composure.
- mechanism: Guidance need not be only verbal indication: an unstable traveler can be accompanied bodily until a composed bearing becomes possible.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Being off the intended way supplies the travel instability that requires assistance.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B008: Supported, swaying walking turns guidance into accompaniment that bears weakness.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B010: Calm and composed bearing supplies the embodied result of that accompaniment.

## cognitive recollection
- reading: It can exploratorily indicate unretained or unformulated knowledge that guidance renders apprehensible and clear.
- mechanism: A cognitive rather than condemnatory reading becomes live: what was not retained or articulated is apprehended and clarified into a communicable way.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B004: Loss of retention or forgetting supplies a deficit of held knowledge.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Perceptual or mental finding supplies apprehension of what was unavailable.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Clarifying a truth or way turns recovered apprehension into intelligible direction.

## night cover to disclosure
- reading: Hiddenness becomes a temporary covered phase whose reversal into visibility is itself part of what هَدَىٰ accomplishes.
- mechanism: The opening alternation between exposed, clear morning and settled, covering night turns the focus transition into a visibility cycle: ضَالًّا is a covered phase, finding is disclosure, and guidance is dawnlike legibility.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Encounter supplies the moment at which the covered subject becomes discoverable.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness supplies the focus state that the light-dark context can modulate.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Clarification of a way supplies the transition into legibility.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight and visibility gives guidance a disclosure image.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night darkness supplies the contrasting field in which a subject can be obscured.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B001: Settled stillness with the night closing over things makes concealment a phase rather than abandonment.

## nonabandoning cultivation
- reading: Guidance is the course-forming labor of a custodian who neither leaves nor distances the vulnerable subject and instead repairs, raises, and completes.
- mechanism: The denial of leaving and heart-distance, combined with lordly repair, cultivation, and even the split-root image of growth, revises guidance from a one-time correction into sustained custodial formation.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies active attention to one who could otherwise remain unlocated.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B003: The lost condition supplies the vulnerability to abandonment that the context explicitly negates.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Giving a course supplies the formative outcome of continued care.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and relinquishing name the relation explicitly denied, so finding remains ongoing custody.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, upbringing, and completion make guidance a developmental practice.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant growth and nourishment image deepens cultivation while remaining a form-distant activation.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Hatred that distances the heart supplies the second rejected mode of relation.

## later beneficial trajectory
- reading: فَهَدَىٰ opens a directed trajectory whose later good, provision, and satisfaction can disclose what the initial guidance was doing.
- mechanism: The later-over-first comparison and promised giving toward satisfaction stretch هَدَىٰ across time: it initiates a course whose beneficial outcome is not exhausted by the moment of being found.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Deviation from aim supplies the trajectory's initial misalignment.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction let guidance extend beyond a point event.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time opens a temporal horizon for the guided course.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies the value toward which the course tends.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an outcome or final consequence makes the first phase readable from its eventual issue.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Giving supplies concrete provision along the course rather than direction alone.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction opposed to discontent marks an affective fruition of the trajectory.

## close unbroken following
- reading: The exploratory split-root resonance makes guidance the close, gapless continuation of being found under non-abandoning care.
- mechanism: The non-abandonment cue and the non-dominant w-l-y images of nearness and close succession activate the fa in فَهَدَىٰ as care following the found condition without a relational gap.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: The encounter supplies the first event in the closely linked pair.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course-giving supplies the action that follows encounter as its immediate continuation.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Relinquishment, under explicit negation, rules out a break between encounter and care.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: The split-root image of nearness without an interval activates continuity across the transition.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Close succession of one thing after another supplies a temporal image for the focus fa.

## parallel need remedy
- reading: 93:7 is the middle case in a care matrix where each exposed lack is noticed and met with the provision proper to it.
- mechanism: The repeated found-you/state/fa-remedy pattern places ضَالًّا between orphaned exposure answered by shelter and material need answered by sufficiency; it becomes one vulnerable condition met by its fitting provision, not an isolated verdict.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies the repeated diagnostic encounter shared by the three recollections.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Being off aim is the middle condition in the parallel need-remedy series.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Guidance is the remedy fitted specifically to disorientation.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: The earlier repeated finding establishes the shared frame for vulnerable conditions.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance of a child from a caretaker supplies the first relational deficit.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering refuge supplies the matching repair for severed care.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: The later repeated finding closes the structural frame around the focus.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the third deficit in the parallel series.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the fitting repair for material lack.

## bewilderment as destitution
- reading: The lost one may lack navigational capacity or sufficiency, so guidance equips movement rather than merely supplying an answer.
- mechanism: The third triad's ع ي ل inventory unexpectedly includes the bewilderment of a stray about its destination; under identical syntax, that image back-activates ضَالًّا as navigational bewilderment and lets sufficiency mean provision of capacity for the way.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B003: Failure to locate a fixed place anchors the focus condition in navigational loss.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction provide what destination-bewilderment lacks.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Repeated finding licenses the neighboring branch image to echo back into the focus frame.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B008: A stray's confusion about its destination closely mirrors the focus problem of unlocated direction.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency recasts the remedy as furnishing enough capacity to proceed.

## noncoercive drawn open
- reading: Guidance is constrained by its social afterlife: it opens a channel and draws forth gently, refusing the power that crushes or verbally drives away.
- mechanism: The paired prohibitions exclude domination from above and harsh verbal repulsion; the n-h-r opening/flow image and the split s-l-l images of gentle extraction and smooth flow then activate a positive mode in which guidance opens passage and draws the vulnerable traveler without crushing.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: The off-course condition anchors the social mode under discussion to the focus traveler.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication supplies the focus-side alternative to force.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Loss of a caretaker identifies the vulnerable person on whom guiding power must not bear down.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Dominance from above that humiliates the overpowered supplies the rejected coercive mode.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Question and request identify another person whose lack calls for responsive direction.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The split-root image of drawing out gently and quietly supplies an exploratory noncoercive motion.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B006: Smooth flowing supplies continuity without violent displacement.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the explicitly prohibited speech mode.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until flow is possible supplies a cross-branch positive counterimage to rebuff.

## guidance made tellable
- reading: The transition is also narratable guidance: speaking it as received favor renews and manifests a way for a wider social field.
- mechanism: The final imperative gathers the guided transition as a favorable condition to be manifested in renewing speech; guidance moves from private past event to a tellable event that can become newly present for hearers.
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B002: Existence or becoming present lets the found event be treated as something made manifest.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Clarification of a way supplies the content that can be made available to others.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Favorable condition and blessing classify the transition as received good rather than self-produced mastery.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, upbringing, and completion preserve the formative agent inside the narrated event.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B001: Coming to be after not being supplies eventfulness and renewed presence.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as report makes the guidance transition tellable.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something out and displaying it supplies the public manifestation of received guidance.

## pastoral recovery and consecration
- reading: As a contained material analogy, it can stage recovery of an exposed stray into custody and a protected destination.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding a sought or lost thing supplies recovery into custody.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B005: A stray animal exposed in a wasteland supplies the vulnerable material scene.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B005: An offering led to sanctuary changes bare retrieval into protected, purposive leading.

## concealed affect met as gift
- reading: As an affective resonance, guidance can answer a concealed interior of sorrow or longing as a gracious gift of nearness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B004: Inward sorrow or love gives the encounter an affective interior.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness makes that interior affect unmanifest before the transition.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B004: A gracious gift sent to someone loved or close renders guidance as an intimate answering gift.

## renewal after severance
- reading: As a contained split-root resonance, the transition can feel like a newly constituted course emerging after interruption or obscurity.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B004: Newness arising after a cut supplies the exploratory image of renewed emergence.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness supplies the interval or obscured condition from which renewal emerges.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: An intended course gives renewed emergence a direction rather than mere novelty.


# Focus 93:8

## need to sufficiency
- reading: He encountered you in real need and causally moved you into a condition in which need no longer ruled.
- mechanism: Finding locates an actual condition of poverty and need; the fa-linked causative then reduces the addressee's dependence and establishes means. The center is not an abstract label but the condition on which the final intervention acts.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and coming upon supplies detection of the actual condition and opens the state-to-remedy sequence.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the deficient state that the final causative changes.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B001: Wealth and reduced need supply the resulting condition in which lack no longer governs.

## capacity for dependents
- reading: The verse repairs a gap between dependents and means by making the addressee able to carry support.
- mechanism: The focus can describe a mismatch between obligations and capacity: one is responsible for dependents without enough means, then is made adequate to support them. Enrichment is functional carrying capacity, not merely a larger private balance.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B003: Capacity, means, and wealth make the opening verb resonate with the resource dimension diagnosed as missing.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B002: Dependents under one's support turn the middle state into a relational burden of care rather than solitary poverty.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and standing in for what is absent supply the outcome as adequate support capacity.

## directionless to capable
- reading: عَائِلًا can also expose directionless or overwhelmed incapacity, and أَغْنَىٰ can mark being made practically able to proceed.
- mechanism: Need can be practical disorientation or incapacity: the subject is unable to find a viable direction or is overmatched by what confronts him. The outcome is enough means, substitution, or competence to proceed.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Coming upon a lost thing supplies the encounter with a subject whose viable way is not yet secured.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B008: A stray creature unable to choose a direction supplies a spatial image for the focus state.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B009: Being overwhelmed supplies the functional incapacity that makes directionlessness consequential.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing, availing, and replacing supply enough capability to overcome the impasse.

## need across visibility and cover
- reading: Need is recognized whether exposed or covered, and sufficiency names care that remains through both phases.
- mechanism: The opening alternation between emergence in clear light and a still, covering night revises finding as recognition across changing visibility. The need in 93:8 need not become real only when exposed; the resulting sufficiency is care that persists when the condition is manifest and when it is covered.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and coming upon anchor the revised model in the focus act of recognizing a condition.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing supplies the durable counter-state that can hold across changes in visibility.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight and appearance supply the phase in which a condition becomes plainly detectable.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night and its darkness supply the contrasting phase in which the same condition may not be outwardly visible.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth turns nocturnal stillness into an image of hidden rather than absent need.

## nonabandoning nurture
- reading: Enrichment is sustained, non-repellent nurture that completes what the needy person cannot yet supply.
- mechanism: Negated leaving and heart-distancing flank the image of lordly repair, upbringing, and completion. Against that field, فَأَغْنَىٰ becomes the material face of sustained nurture: the needy state is not answered by a detached payment but by a relation that neither releases nor repels.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the vulnerable condition requiring more than a momentary transfer.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and standing in supply the focus outcome as reliable provision.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and releasing are explicitly negated, defining the transition out of need as non-abandonment.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, upbringing, and completion supply the longitudinal process by which sufficiency is produced.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Heart-distancing hatred is also negated, excluding emotionally repellent provision from the care mechanism.

## sufficiency as unfolding outcome
- reading: فَأَغْنَىٰ initiates an adequacy whose beneficial force can unfold from an earlier condition toward a better outcome.
- mechanism: The contrast of later and first, joined to beneficial good and the return of a thing to its outcome, stretches the compact fa-sequence into a trajectory. What begins as relief from need can mature into a better, more useful adequacy whose value is visible at its outcome.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the initial condition from which the trajectory departs.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and availing define the outcome by usefulness rather than by quantity alone.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness after a first phase supplies the temporal comparison within which adequacy can improve.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good makes the later outcome functionally better, not merely chronologically later.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an eventual outcome supplies maturation from first condition to realized consequence.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The split target's one-thing-after-another image supplies staged continuity between the first state and what follows.

## gift calibrated to satisfaction
- reading: He provisioned you in a way measured by actual sufficiency and settled recipient satisfaction.
- mechanism: Giving, nurturing completion, and satisfaction form a relational measure of enough. The result of أَغْنَىٰ is therefore not defined only by the amount transferred; it is provision that actually reaches adequacy for the recipient.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Need supplies the recipient-side deficit against which a gift must be measured.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and availing define enrichment by whether it genuinely answers the lack.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handing over and giving supply the transfer mechanism rather than an unexplained change of status.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing and bringing to completion make the gift responsive to development over time.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance and satisfaction supply the recipient-side sign that provision has reached enough.

## support network repair
- reading: A person situated in unsupported dependency is gathered into, and made adequate within, a functioning support ecology.
- mechanism: The immediately preceding parallel maps finding an orphaned state to gathering into shelter. Because orphanhood is severance from a caregiver, it makes the focus's need relational: أَغْنَىٰ can repair a support network and confer capacity within it, not merely add cash to an isolated individual.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and coming upon anchor 93:8 in the same diagnostic action as the preceding parallel.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B002: Dependents under support make the focus lack a question of sustaining relationships.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and standing in for an absence supply repaired support capacity.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: The repeated finding branch establishes an explicit parallel state-diagnosis frame.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child cut off from a caregiver supplies the structural form of unsupported vulnerability.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering place that shelters supplies relational reattachment as the counter-state.

## guided capacity
