- reading: The adjacent lost-to-guided parallel makes a coexisting reading live: he found you directionless or overmatched and made you sufficiently capable to proceed.
- mechanism: The preceding parallel gives the baseline's directionless branch a striking echo: a lost creature in a place of loss is answered by gentle direction. The focus can then retain an economic reading while also describing the granting of enough practical orientation and capacity to move beyond an impasse.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B008: A stray creature unable to choose its direction supplies the focus's exploratory spatial state.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B009: Being overwhelmed supplies the incapacity that orientation and means must overcome.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and availing supply enough functional competence to proceed.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Repeated finding binds the context's lost state to the focus's neighboring state.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B005: A stray thing in a place of loss closely echoes the focus root's directionless stray image.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a road supplies the context's directional remedy.

## sufficiency without domination
- reading: Received sufficiency creates a duty not to convert capacity into dominance over those whose support has been severed.
- mechanism: The first command after the recollections reverses the enriched person's position: one who has received capacity must not turn that elevation into pressure upon someone cut off from a caregiver. Enrichment therefore carries an anti-domination obligation toward structurally unsupported people.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B002: Dependents under support keep the focus state relational and expose who remains vulnerable to unequal power.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency and means supply the new position of capacity whose use is ethically constrained.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caregiver identifies the person toward whom received sufficiency must remain protective.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Domination from above that humiliates the subdued is explicitly prohibited, blocking sufficiency from becoming coercive rank.

## open channel for request
- reading: Sufficiency is porous capacity: it receives another's request gently and keeps an answerable flow open.
- mechanism: Asking, fulfillment of a request, negated harsh rebuke, and an opening that allows flow recast sufficiency as porous capacity. The person made sufficient is not a closed reservoir; the gift remains socially operative by letting another's need be voiced and answered without humiliation.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need anchor the later request in the same field of lack as the focus.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and standing in supply capacity that can answer another person's lack.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Questioning and requesting supply the social act through which hidden need reaches the sufficient person.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B003: Fulfilling a request supplies the live possibility that sufficiency becomes responsive action.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The split target's gentle drawing-out image supplies a non-coercive manner of receiving what the asker discloses.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke is explicitly negated, preventing the holder of means from silencing the request.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until something flows supply the counter-image of keeping provision and speech unobstructed.

## blessing becomes voice
- reading: Enrichment can be a nurtured good condition that converts inward experience into renewed, manifest, and shareable voice.
- mechanism: The final consequence names a good condition as a nurtured blessing and commands it to become renewed speech and manifest report. This activates the focus root's vocal branch: enrichment can include the recovery of a voice by which inward lack and its transformation become publicly tellable.
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B004: Inward sorrow or love supplies an affective condition that may initially remain unvoiced.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B003: Song and vocal modulation supply a non-material mode in which enrichment becomes audible expression.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and blessing identify the transformed state as a benefit rather than mere possession.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion identify the blessing as cultivated by the same sustaining relation.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as news supplies the form through which the transformed condition is repeatedly told.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing forth and making manifest turns an inwardly held benefit into public witness.

## supported sway
- reading: As a contained bodily analogy, he found you moving unsteadily and supplied enough support for the movement to continue.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B003: Swaying or unsteady gait supplies a bodily image of the focus deficiency.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and standing in supply the support that makes continued motion possible.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B008: Walking with reliance and sway shows that guided movement may be supported without erasing bodily instability.

## renewal after severance
- reading: As a mapping-derived analogy, a cut and exposed phase is followed by renewed, settled habitability within a recurring day-night order.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B004: Newness arising after a cut supplies renewal as the opening edge of the focus transformation.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B005: A horse released into the wilderness supplies severance, exposure, and lack of a containing home.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B004: Settled dwelling supplies the counter-state in which exposed wandering becomes habitable continuity.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B001: The extension of morning through the day supplies one phase of recurring temporal renewal.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night as the contrary of day supplies the alternating phase that makes renewal cyclical rather than singular.

## household protection
- reading: As a contained social resonance, enrichment can mean being placed within a protective household capable of carrying dependents.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B002: Dependents under one's support supply the household field in which need and responsibility coexist.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B006: Marriage as sufficiency or protection supplies a possible form of household-based security.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caregiver supplies the relational deficit that a protective household counters.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering place that shelters supplies emplacement inside a protective social unit.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: Ward and stepchild kinship supplies a concrete household-care relation rather than abstract ownership.


# Focus 93:9

## protection gap not power license
- reading: Do not turn the orphan's missing protection into a license for vertical power; the very asymmetry that enables coercion intensifies the duty of restraint.
- mechanism: Loss of the ordinary protector creates an asymmetry in which coercion is unusually easy. The prohibition blocks the conversion of that protection gap into permission to compel, humiliate, or take without consent.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Cut-off offspring supplies the missing-protector condition that exposes the orphan to another's unchecked power.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Overpowering from above supplies the prohibited operation: making vulnerability answer to force, humiliation, or nonconsensual taking.

## solitude not social lowliness
- reading: It also exposes a mechanism: never convert someone's aloneness or lack of peers into evidence that they may be made socially lower.
- mechanism: Social isolation removes peers and witnesses. The command therefore resists a second injury in which being alone is interpreted as being lower, disposable, or available for domination.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B002: Isolated or peerless singleness supplies the social exposure of one who stands without an equivalent beside them.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Subduing from above turns horizontal aloneness into an imposed vertical inferiority, the move the prohibition interrupts.

## withheld care as quiet qahr
- reading: Do not dominate quietly by postponing needed care and making the orphan wait beneath another's controlling discretion.
- mechanism: Domination need not be an overt blow. A guardian can preserve leverage by delaying care that the dependent cannot obtain elsewhere; withholding then performs qahr through time.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B004: Delayed motion or delayed kindness makes lateness of care an internal feature of the orphan's exposure.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Superior coercive power supplies the function of strategic delay: maintaining dependence until the weaker party yields.

## respite for person not delay of duty
- reading: Give time to the orphan but do not give time to your own obligation: neither rush the person coercively nor defer the care they need.
- mechanism: The context branch of gentle forbearance distinguishes two superficially similar delays: time generously granted to the vulnerable person and care selfishly withheld by the powerful person. Non-qahr regulates pace in both directions.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B004: Delayed kindness supplies the harmful lag in which an orphan's need remains unanswered.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Coercion from above supplies the danger of either making the orphan wait or forcing the orphan to move at the guardian's pace.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B006: Gentleness and forbearance supply a positive tempo: patient space is given to the person rather than extracted from their need.

## cover must shelter not conceal control
- reading: It also tests private protection: an orphan's enclosure must shelter them, not make hidden control easier or their protest less audible.
- mechanism: Isolation plus darkness and covering creates a private enclosure with few witnesses. The same enclosure can be shelter or concealment; the focus prohibition decides against using privacy to hide control or impose silence.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B002: Isolated singleness supplies the absence of peers or witnesses around the vulnerable person.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Subjugating power supplies the hidden act that an unwitnessed custodial space could enable.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's darkness supplies reduced visibility around the relation of protector and protected.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth supplies the ambivalent enclosure that may protect a person or conceal what is done to them.

## entrusted cultivation not reenacted abandonment
- reading: Treat the orphan as an entrusted life whose first rupture must not be reenacted: repair, raise, and grow rather than possess, expel, or withdraw the heart.
- mechanism: The preceding denial of leaving and heart-distancing turns the orphan's original severance into something the addressee must not repeat. Images of entrusted deposit, repair, upbringing, and growth make the positive alternative custodial cultivation rather than possessive rule.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Severance from the protecting parent supplies the rupture that harmful custody could either repair or repeat.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Possessive power from above supplies the wrong response to someone placed within another's care.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and relinquishing supply the direct pattern of abandonment that the context denies.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B005: Deposit and entrustment recast custody as answerability for what is held, not ownership of it.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, upbringing, and completion supply the constructive function that should replace qahr.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant mapped branch of nourishment and growth sharpens cultivation as an alternative to keeping the dependent small.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Hatred that distances the heart supplies emotional abandonment, not merely physical expulsion.

## guardianship ordered to future agency
- reading: Exercise no authority that locks the orphan into dependence; any guardianship must be temporary, beneficiary-first, and ordered toward a better later condition.
- mechanism: The sequence of later, beneficial outcome, and taking charge activates a temporal test for guardianship. Present authority is legitimate only as provisional administration directed toward the orphan's later good and eventual priority, not as a permanent rank.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The child cut off before maturity supplies a vulnerable present state that should not be frozen into a permanent identity.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Dominance from above supplies the danger that temporary stewardship becomes durable subordination.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Later-after-earlier supplies a trajectory beyond the orphan's present dependency.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the criterion by which present custodial action is judged.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome supplies the endpoint toward which intervention must be ordered.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B003: The non-dominant mapped image of taking charge supplies provisional administration rather than ownership.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B008: The non-dominant mapped image of priority supplies the beneficiary's claim to outrank the guardian's convenience.

## giving without seizure toward assent
- reading: Reverse the flow of power: transfer benefit and growing agency toward the orphan in a manner responsive to their welfare and assent, rather than using aid itself as coercion.
- mechanism: The context reverses the direction of power: the superior gives rather than forcibly takes, reparative nurture rather than dominance structures the relation, and satisfaction can be mutual rather than imposed. Non-qahr thus acquires a consent-sensitive and agency-transferring shape.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The missing-protector condition supplies the unequal relation in which the recipient has limited bargaining power.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Forced taking without consent supplies the prohibited direction and manner of transfer.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handing over and giving reverse qahr's extractive flow by moving benefit toward the dependent.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair and upbringing make the transfer developmental rather than a display of patronal control.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Mutual satisfaction supplies an interactional test: aid should not erase the weaker party's assent.

## received shelter becomes active repair
- reading: It asks the formerly sheltered addressee to relay shelter: notice the protection gap, gather the isolated person into safety, and let mercy rather than superiority define that inclusion.
- mechanism: The exact orphan condition reappears inside a found-and-sheltered sequence. This supplies a reversible pattern: one who received gathering and compassion when cut off must not merely avoid harm but actively close another orphan's protection gap.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The focus orphan's severance supplies the present protection gap to be answered.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Overpowering supplies the rejected use of the stronger party's position.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Finding and encountering supply recognition: vulnerability is first noticed rather than passed by.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: The repeated cut-off-child image identifies the addressee's remembered condition with the focus object's condition.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Gathering into a sheltering side supplies the spatial reversal of isolation.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy toward one pitied supplies the affective manner of shelter, excluding humiliating patronage.

## correction by gentle direction not humiliation
- reading: Even when the orphan seems heedless or off-course, do not turn correction into humiliation or threat; first recognize, then indicate a way gently.
- mechanism: A latent focus branch permits perceived heedlessness or shortcoming to enter the orphan image. The context answers lostness with gentle indication of a way, while a split branch keeps threat as a live rejected alternative. The result is a pedagogical reading of non-qahr.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B003: Heedlessness or falling short supplies the perceived deficiency by which a guardian might rationalize harsh correction.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Humiliating compulsion supplies the prohibited educational method.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies attentive recognition before any attempt to redirect.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Being off the intended way supplies the condition needing direction rather than punishment.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward road and truth supplies the constructive alternative to coercive correction.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B010: The non-dominant mapped image of threat and intimidation preserves the rejected alternative against which gentle guidance becomes vivid.

## dependency must move toward sufficiency
- reading: Do not make provision a technology of control; answer material exposure in ways that move the orphan toward sufficiency and reduce the provider's leverage.
- mechanism: Poverty makes a person materially answerable to whoever controls provision. The context's found-poor→made-sufficient sequence therefore turns non-qahr into an economic trajectory: care should reduce the leverage of need instead of preserving it.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Loss of a protecting provider supplies the material as well as social exposure of the orphan.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Dominance and compelled taking supply the power that scarcity gives a provider over a dependent.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies recognition of material vulnerability as a condition requiring response.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and want supply the dependency through which provision can become coercive leverage.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the positive endpoint: enoughness that reduces dependence on another's arbitrary will.

## parallel need without verbal expulsion
- reading: Do not make need recoil through voice or posture: receive the structurally exposed orphan with the same non-expelling attention owed to an audible requester.
- mechanism: The exact parallel construction places structural vulnerability beside voiced need and places qahr beside rough rebuke. This makes speech one mechanism by which a stronger party can reproduce the orphan's social smallness; a split branch offers gentle drawing as an exploratory counter-motion.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The missing protector supplies unvoiced structural need.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Humbling from above supplies the broader social action that harsh speech can enact.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Question and request supply need made audible, placing the response under an interactional test.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The non-dominant mapped image of drawing out gently and quietly supplies an exploratory opposite to expelling the needy speaker.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Rebuking with coarse speech supplies the verbal form of making a vulnerable person recoil.

## soft care as visible testimony
- reading: Soft, reparative treatment of the orphan is itself visible evidence of received care, though any public telling must preserve the orphan's dignity rather than display them.
- mechanism: Softness, reparative nurture, and making something manifest turn the treatment of the orphan into evidence of how received care has been understood. Non-qahr is not only private abstention; it can make grace publicly legible without turning the orphan into an exhibit.
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The orphan's protection gap supplies the concrete relation in which claimed gratitude is tested.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Humiliating dominance supplies conduct that would contradict any account of received care.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B002: Softness, ease, and well-being supply the texture that remembered blessing should reproduce in care.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair and upbringing supply the sustained practice through which softness becomes more than sentiment.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Making a thing appear and become manifest supplies public legibility to the care relation.

## heat extraction from the already depleted
- reading: As a contained material analogy, do not subject an already depleted person to heat-like pressure that extracts their remaining money, labor, confession, or compliance; care should restore sufficiency.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Cut-off offspring supplies the person whose protective reserves are already reduced.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B002: Flesh seized by fire until its juices run supplies a material model of pressure that extracts remaining substance while changing its subject.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B004: Poor nourishment supplies the already-depleted condition that makes further extraction especially destructive.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the reversal: restore usable substance rather than press until the remainder leaks away.

## preserve peerless form without grinding
- reading: As a contained formative analogy, do not grind the orphan's peerless form into conformity; cultivation may clarify and polish, but it must not pulverize distinctiveness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B002: Peerless singleness supplies a distinctive form that care should not erase merely because it lacks a matching peer.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B004: A hard or smooth grinding stone supplies crushing standardization as the prohibited formative pressure.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B007: Polishing a blade or heart supplies a contrasting form of care that clarifies without pulverizing the underlying form.

## do not drive the orphan backward
- reading: As contained branch wordplay, do not drive an already delayed life backward; protect a gentle, beneficial path from interrupted dependence toward a later condition.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B004: Delayed motion and delayed care supply an already impeded trajectory.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B005: Backward retreat supplies the outlier's directional image of authority reversing another's progress.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Later-after-earlier supplies a forward temporal horizon against which forced regression becomes visible.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies the quality required of forward movement.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a way supplies the noncoercive forward alternative.


# Focus 93:10

## person over demand
- reading: The command protects the asker's human standing during an asymmetrical request, even when the requested thing cannot be supplied.
- mechanism: An approach made through asking meets a speech act capable of driving the asker back. The prohibition protects the asker's standing at the moment of dependence; it constrains refusal and manner without by itself promising that every request will be granted.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking and requesting supply the vulnerable approach and identify the person whose treatment is regulated.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the prohibited force that can repel and demean the approaching asker.

## request channel
- reading: It also regulates the social channel through which a need becomes answer: do not make that channel cut, wound, or eject the person using it.
- mechanism: The focus can be modeled as a channel between articulated need and possible response. The river image adds movement but also cutting and erosion, while widening-until-flow adds access; negation therefore warns against making the response channel itself wound or sweep aside the requester.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B002: The sought request supplies the content that is trying to pass from need into response.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B003: Fulfilling a request supplies a possible endpoint while leaving room for responses short of fulfillment.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B001: A river cutting ground supplies the image of a response channel that can carry provision yet erode social ground.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until flow supply the alternative possibility of keeping access passable rather than closing it by rebuke.

## reciprocal questioning
- reading: The asker may also be an interlocutor whose question creates a reciprocal obligation to remain answerable without verbal expulsion.
- mechanism: The apparently one-way petitioner can be heard as a participant in a question-and-answer relation. Rebuke is then not only discourtesy but a unilateral closure of reciprocity by the party with greater authority.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B004: Reciprocal questioning supplies a two-sided exchange latent within the figure of the asker.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the speech act by which the stronger participant can shut that exchange down.

## cover to exposure
- reading: Do not punish disclosure by daylighting the asker or converting a privately revealed need into public exposure.
- mechanism: The opening movement from solar emergence to settled, covering night activates the daylight branch inside the focus verb. Rebuke can therefore be heard as coercive exposure: forcing a need that approached under cover into public glare. The prohibition acquires a privacy function alongside its ordinary speech function.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking supplies the potentially exposing act of making a need known.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B002: Day opening with light supplies the latent exposure pole carried by the prohibited verb.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight supplies visible disclosure at the bright end of the contextual contrast.
  - 93:2 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night and darkness supply the opposed condition in which visibility recedes.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth supplies concealment as a protective rather than merely absent state.

## nonabandoning response
- reading: Rebuke is objectionable because it converts a response into abandonment, expulsion, and affective withdrawal.
- mechanism: The negated abandonment and aversive distancing of 93:3, set against nurture and completion, make rebuke a relational action: it can cast a requester out before any material decision is made. The response must not reproduce abandonment in miniature.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The request supplies the moment at which continued relation is tested.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the immediate verbal mechanism of driving someone back.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B001: Leaving and letting go supply the abandoned relation that the context explicitly negates.
  - 93:3 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and bringing to completion supply the positive relational counterforce.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B002: Casting or driving outward supplies the spatial effect that a rebuke can have on the requester.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Aversion that distances the heart supplies the affective withdrawal concealed inside dismissal.

## deferred without distance
- reading: It also governs deferred response: inability to answer now must not be communicated as final expulsion or relational distance.
- mechanism: The later/better/earlier comparison introduces a time horizon into the request. A beneficial answer may be delayed, but the split inventory of the earlier term also activates return-to-outcome and proximity without a gap. Thus non-fulfillment now need not become rejection: delay should preserve nearness, attention, and a route to an outcome.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B002: The sought request supplies an unmet object whose answer may unfold over time.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the false closure that can make postponement feel like rejection.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies a legitimate temporal gap between request and outcome.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the criterion by which a later answer could exceed an immediate one.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an eventual outcome supplies continuity between the first request and its later resolution.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Proximity without an intervening gap supplies the relational nearness that should survive delay.

## gift horizon
- reading: Withholding rebuke is the minimum condition of a response process oriented toward fitting help and restored satisfaction, though not an unconditional grant.
- mechanism: Giving, nurturing completion, and reciprocal satisfaction activate a positive horizon behind the negative command. The encounter should remain oriented toward a fitting response that can restore satisfaction, while the grammar still stops short of requiring the grant of every requested object.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B002: The sought object supplies what the asker hopes will be answered.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B003: Fulfillment of a request supplies the strongest possible endpoint without making it automatic.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the response mode that would contradict this constructive horizon.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handing over and giving supply a concrete response rather than a merely verbal absence of harm.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion supply a process calibrated to what actually repairs the asker's condition.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Mutual satisfaction supplies a relational measure of response beyond unilateral disposal of the case.

## refuge threshold
- reading: The asker may be a person testing whether a sheltering social threshold is open; rebuke would close that threshold and repeat severance.
- mechanism: Being found in severed dependence and then gathered into refuge recasts the asker as someone at a social threshold. Rebuke is functionally an expulsion from possible shelter; non-rebuke keeps the threshold traversable and permits mercy to gather rather than scatter.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The request supplies the threshold-crossing approach toward someone able to respond.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Driving back with harsh speech supplies the verbal form of exclusion from that threshold.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Encountering and finding supply the moment when hidden vulnerability becomes another party's responsibility.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance from a guardian supplies the social disconnection that makes access to refuge consequential.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering place that shelters supplies the spatial reversal of being driven away.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B002: Tender mercy toward one who evokes concern supplies the affective mode of receiving the approach.

## epistemic gentleness
- reading: The asker can coexist as a knowledge-seeker whose uncertainty calls for gentle orientation rather than shaming.
- mechanism: The found-straying-guided sequence activates an epistemic asker: one who asks because direction is missing. Since guidance is explicitly gentle in its branch inventory, the prohibition governs pedagogy as well as almsgiving; uncertainty is to be met with direction, not punished by verbal force.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking supplies inquiry for information or direction as well as request for a material object.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the punitive answer that can make admitted uncertainty unsafe.
  - 93:7 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding someone supplies recognition of the questioner's actual location before direction is offered.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B001: Straying from guidance or aim supplies the directional deficit that can generate a question.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward path and truth supplies the constructive mode of answering disorientation.

## sufficiency access
- reading: Not rebuking preserves access through which an identified insufficiency, including the needs of dependents, may move toward adequacy.
- mechanism: The found-need-sufficiency sequence makes asking a diagnostic signal of a sufficiency gap, possibly carrying dependents beyond the visible speaker. Rebuke is not only harmful speech; it can close access to the very relation through which scarcity becomes adequacy.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B002: The sought request supplies an articulated lack rather than an abstract social category.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Driving back with harsh speech supplies a way of closing access before need is assessed.
  - 93:8 **وَوَجَدَكَ** و ج د B001: Finding supplies attentive detection of need rather than waiting for it to become louder.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and want supply the concrete deficit that can stand behind a request.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B002: Dependents and those one supports supply a hidden downstream scope beyond the visible asker.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the remedial endpoint without reducing it to wealth alone.

## power mirror
- reading: Rebuke is wrong because, in this asymmetry, speech can enact domination from above and convert need into humiliation.
- mechanism: The nearly mirrored constructions for the orphan and the asker transfer the vertical force of domination into the interpretation of rebuke. Harsh speech is prohibited not merely for unpleasantness but because it lets the socially higher party press down, humiliate, and sever a vulnerable person's access.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: The act of asking supplies an asymmetry in which one party exposes dependence to another.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal driving-back supplies the asker's member of the paired prohibitions.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Earlier severance from a guardian supplies remembered vulnerability before it becomes an ethical category.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: The repeated severed-dependent category supplies the counterpart placed in parallel with the asker.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Conquest from above that subdues and humiliates supplies the power geometry transferred across the syntactic mirror.

## generous speech
- reading: The sequence invites replacement speech: soft, answerable, and generative language through which received benefit can continue toward another.
- mechanism: The final command does not leave the response ethic at silence: softness and nurture culminate in renewed, revealing speech. Placed after the ban on rebuke, it activates a positive replacement mode in which speech keeps benefit circulating rather than using voice to eject the asker.
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B004: Reciprocal questioning supplies an exchange that requires an answerable voice rather than mere silence.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the destructive speech mode that the following imperative can replace.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B002: Softness, ease, and well-being supply the texture of a non-rebuking response.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion supply a purpose for speech beyond self-display.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as news supplies an ongoing, generative alternative to a terminating rebuke.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something forth and making it visible supplies communicative disclosure of benefit.

## delicate extraction
- reading: Asking may delicately draw a concealed need into view; rebuke violates that emergence by turning it into a sudden seizure of control.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Gentle, concealed drawing-out supplies an image for a question or request bringing hidden need into speech.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B006: A sudden or stealthy snatch supplies the violent reversal by which the response seizes control of a delicate disclosure.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke keeps the material analogy attached to the actual prohibited speech act.

## guidance threat split
- reading: Guidance can still violate the command when correctness is delivered as threat; gentle direction and intimidating answer remain sharply distinct.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking supplies the appeal for direction that makes delivery mode consequential.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke supplies the prohibited mode into which even a substantively correct answer can collapse.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward path and truth supplies guidance as a non-coercive answer.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B010: Threat and intimidation supply the live contrary mode exposed by the cue's non-dominant mapped inventory.

## dependency chain
- reading: The visible asker may carry a hidden dependency chain, so rebuke can propagate exclusion beyond the person standing at the door.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B005: Chainlike connection supplies a model in which the visible request is linked to people and needs not present in the encounter.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B001: A watercourse cutting ground supplies the possibility that one dismissive response fractures a wider support route.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B002: Dependents and those one maintains supply the hidden human links carried by a single asker's need.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the network-level outcome whose access may be affected by the response.


# Focus 93:11

## bestowal account
- reading: Give renewed verbal account of bestowed well-being while naming it as your Lord's, not as self-produced property.
- mechanism: Bestowed well-being is verbally renewed as news whose ownership and source remain with the Lord; telling is attribution, not a display of autonomous possession.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Bestowed favor and good condition supply the content to be acknowledged.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and ownership keep the favor attributed to its sovereign source.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Renewed speech and news provide the literal vehicle for acknowledgment.

## manifesting event
- reading: By telling, make the Lord's still-operative nurture newly occur in visibility.
- mechanism: Speech does not merely describe a finished gift: it makes the Lord's ongoing nurture newly present and perceptible in a social field.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and gradual completion make the favor an ongoing process rather than a finished object.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B001: Coming to be after not being lets the telling itself count as a fresh event of the favor.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Manifesting what was not visible gives the speech a performative disclosure function.

## affirmative praise
- reading: Answer the favor with an audible 'yes': renew it as praise, assent, and report at once.
- mechanism: The command recruits the praise-form and affirmative 'yes' branches of ن ع م, so the account becomes an answer of assent to the goodness received.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B003: Praising something as excellent turns the report into evaluative praise.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B004: Affirming with 'yes' supplies a responsive posture toward the gift.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Conversation and renewed report carry that praise and assent into speech.

## recollective polish
- reading: Rehearse the favor as a polishing remembrance that restores the teller's own capacity to perceive the Lord's care.
- mechanism: Recounting can polish a dulled heart or perception: nurture is recollected until the received good becomes clear again, so the first audience of the telling may be the teller.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease make the recovered texture of favor something that can soften perception.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair and completion frame recollection as part of the Lord's formative care.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B007: Polishing a sword or heart supplies the strange but explicit image of speech removing accumulated dullness.

## growth continuation
- reading: Carry the favor further by generating fresh effects through each responsible telling.
- mechanism: The non-dominant growth mapping of رَبّ converges with carrying an act further in ن ع م and new occurrence in ح د ث; telling can therefore continue or increase the favor's effects.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B010: Adding and going further recasts favor as extensible action.
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: The split mapping's increase and elevation image supplies growth without replacing the ordinary Lord-reading.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B001: Fresh occurrence makes each telling a possible new increment rather than a duplicate.

## latency to disclosure
- reading: Bring a preserved favor out of cover when its opening arrives, refusing to mistake latency for abandonment.
- mechanism: Morning emergence after a covering, stilled night, followed by the denial of abandonment and heart-distance, makes disclosure a timed emergence from latency: what was covered was not absent or discarded.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Manifestation anchors the changed reading directly in the focus imperative.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B002: Emergence into the sun supplies the opening phase in which a preserved favor becomes visible.
  - 93:2 **سَجَىٰ** س ج و B002: Covering with a cloth gives the preceding quiet interval a containing rather than annihilating function.
  - 93:3 **وَدَّعَكَ** و د ع B005: Deposit and entrustment make non-abandonment compatible with something held in safekeeping.
  - 93:3 **قَلَىٰ** ق ل ي B003: Heart-distancing aversion, explicitly negated, blocks a reading of silence as rejection.

## open ended growth
- reading: Tell the favor as an open trajectory—first stage, present increment, and beneficial issue still becoming.
- mechanism: The later being more beneficial than the first, together with outcome and uninterrupted succession images, turns favor into an unfinished trajectory whose account can register growth and destination.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B010: Going further supplies the focus-side capacity for favor to exceed its current state.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B001: New occurrence lets the account register increments still arriving.
  - 93:4 **وَلَلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Later-after-first supplies the temporal comparison that prevents a closed inventory.
  - 93:4 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good gives the later movement a qualitative direction rather than mere chronology.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome or final issue lets the first stage be read through what it is becoming.
  - 93:4 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The split mapping's uninterrupted succession gives the trajectory a step-after-step mechanism.

## gift relay
- reading: Relay the received good through speech so that a gift to you becomes usable benefit between persons.
- mechanism: A promised hand-to-hand giving from the nurturing Lord toward satisfaction activates speech as the next transfer in a gift circuit: received benefit is made available to another through intelligible account.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Benefaction reaching another makes favor transferable in effect without changing its divine source.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Report and conversation become the medium of onward delivery.
  - 93:5 **يُعْطِيكَ** ع ط و B002: Handing and giving supply the first transfer in the relay.
  - 93:5 **رَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion identify the giving as formative care, not a detachable commodity.
  - 93:5 **فَتَرْضَىٰٓ** ر ض و B003: Mutual satisfaction supplies a relational horizon for how the relayed account lands.

## transition testimony
- reading: Testify by tracing concrete passages from severance to shelter, hiddenness to direction, and need to sufficiency.
- mechanism: The repeated pattern of encountering a condition and then transforming it supplies the content-form for حَدِّثْ: the favor is narrated as a sequence of before-and-after transitions, not named as a static noun.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Gradual nurture and completion unify the three transformations as one formative mechanism.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Narrated news anchors the resulting testimony form in the focus command.
  - 93:6 **يَجِدْكَ** و ج د B001: Encountering a concrete condition opens the first before-and-after unit.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: Severance from a caregiver gives the initial condition its relational deficit.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a sheltering side supplies the reversal from severance to inclusion.
  - 93:7 **ضَآلًّا** ض ل ل B002: Hiddenness supplies a live condition that is changed by being gently led into a way.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the second transformative result.
  - 93:8 **عَآئِلًا** ع ي ل B001: Poverty and need supply the third exposed condition.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the third result without requiring an absolute-riches reading.

## cohesion after severance
- reading: Reassemble a history of severance into communal memory that can gather rather than isolate.
- mechanism: The focus inventory's scattering image is reversed by the context's severance-and-shelter pair; telling gathers a once-disconnected life into a shareable account and can restore social cohesion.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B008: Departure and scattering expose a negative edge from which favor can be read as recovered cohesion.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B004: Becoming an account carried among people gives reassembled experience a communal memory form.
  - 93:6 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Singularity and severance sharpen the initial condition as social disconnection.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a gathering shelter supplies the counter-motion from dispersion to belonging.

## non dominating testimony
- reading: Tell in a way that cannot crush the unprotected, repel the asker, or convert received favor into rank over them.
- mechanism: The two vulnerable-object prohibitions and the focus command form a three-part pragmatic frame: speech about favor is invalidated if it reproduces domination from above or harsh verbal expulsion.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: Benefaction reaching another keeps the focus on received and transmissible good rather than status.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Actual speech anchors the contextual constraints in the manner and effect of telling.
  - 93:9 **ٱلْيَتِيمَ** ي ت م B001: Loss of a caregiver identifies a hearer or neighbor especially exposed to power.
  - 93:9 **تَقْهَرْ** ق ه ر B001: Humiliating conquest from above supplies the forbidden social geometry.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Asking and seeking place the account beside a person whose need calls for response.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal rebuke supplies the explicitly forbidden speech mode.

## responsive fulfillment
- reading: Let the account be a fitting affirmative answer to inquiry or need, measured by whether it fulfills rather than repels.
- mechanism: Question, requested object, and fulfillment activate the focus utterance as an answer: telling is responsive to a real ask rather than compulsory self-broadcast.
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B004: Affirmative 'yes' gives the favor-account the posture of an answer.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Conversation and report provide the focus-side act of responding.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Question and request supply the initiating address to which telling may answer.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B003: Fulfilling a request makes an adequate answer, not mere talk, the relational goal.

## gentle climate of speech
- reading: Let the account arrive like clear, moisture-bearing air: perceptible and enlivening without verbal violence.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B009: A soft moist south wind supplies a gentle, sustaining atmospheric quality for the telling.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Spoken report is the focus act whose felt climate is being reimagined.
  - 93:1 **وَٱلضُّحَىٰ** ض ح و B005: Clear morning light adds lucidity and opening to the speech climate.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: The ban on harsh rebuke provides the contrast that keeps the imagined wind gentle rather than battering.

## nonextractive disclosure
- reading: Refuse to extract the asker's hidden need; expose your own dependence on received favor instead.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B006: Manifesting something anchors the positive disclosure on the teller's side.
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B001: The teller's bestowed favor, rather than the asker's lack, supplies what is to be exposed.
  - 93:10 **ٱلسَّآئِلَ** س ء ل B001: Gentle hidden extraction supplies the surprising risk of drawing out what belongs to the vulnerable other.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh rebuke identifies the overtly violent version of the same asymmetrical encounter.

## emplaced shelter
- reading: Favor can also be the making of an inhabitable place in which a formerly unprotected person can belong and become sufficient.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B011: A place agreeing with and settling its occupant makes favor an inhabitable fit.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Narrated account lets that acquired belonging become testimony.
  - 93:6 **فَـَٔاوَىٰ** ء و ي B001: Joining a sheltering side provides the concrete transition into belonging.
  - 93:8 **فَأَغْنَىٰ** غ ن ي B004: Sufficiency in a place stabilizes shelter as dwelling rather than temporary cover.

## soothing not din
- reading: The account may fulfill its guidance function by settling the exposed hearer like a lullaby rather than arriving as din or threat.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 93:11 **بِنِعْمَةِ** ن ع م B002: Softness supplies the focus-side quality that a soothing telling preserves.
  - 93:11 **فَحَدِّثْ** ح د ث B003: Actual speech gives the acoustic contrast a direct focus anchor.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B006: Lulling a child to sleep supplies a protective and settling sound image.
  - 93:7 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B004: Loud din supplies the acoustic alternative that would overwhelm rather than settle.
  - 93:10 **تَنْهَرْ** ن ه ر B004: The forbidden harsh rebuke makes the contrast ethically relevant to the final speech command.
